These findings suggest that experts' deliberations may contain forward-looking information that context-aware NLP can extract.

</details>


### [238] [Score the Algebra, Not the Span: Dimension Reduction for Transfer Operator Models of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18918)
*Mark Kozdoba,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有基于谱（Koopman算子主导模态）的降维方法在弱耦合多组件动力学系统中存在"线性遮蔽"问题——要么需要指数级数量的模态，要么完全丢失某个组件。作者提出用坐标生成的σ-代数而非秩来评分，使乘积和幂次免费获得，并引入χ²散度作为嵌入准则，保证2倍内在维度的坐标即可承载算子的完整谱。变分形式下该准则退化为VAMP评分，属同一家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准谱降维（Koopman算子模态）在处理由多个弱相互作用组件组装的系统时，由于每个模态只能支付一个坐标，要么需要指数级模态数，要么整个组件被完全丢弃（而非粗建模），导致该组件任何函数都无法被预测。这一问题被称为"线性遮蔽"（linear masking）。

Method: 提出用坐标生成的σ-代数来评分（而非基于秩），使乘积和幂等操作免费计入。引入嵌入当前与未来之间的χ²散度作为目标准则，并证明2倍内在维度的坐标数足够完成嵌入，其代数可承载算子完整无穷秩谱。变分形式下限制critic为双线性类可恢复VAMP评分，形成统一框架。

Result: 在复合基准系统实验中，秩基方法在k<100的所有秩下完全遗漏被遮蔽组件，而10个代数坐标即可恢复全部组件。代数表示还支持从少量标签预测被遮蔽组件，而直接从高维观测或VAMP特征回归均失败。

Conclusion: 基于σ-代数的χ²散度准则是对抗"线性遮蔽"问题的有效框架，在保持谱完整性的同时以极少量坐标恢复被遮蔽组件，且与现有VAMP方法统一在同一变分家族中，为多组件动力学系统的降维提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dimension reduction for dynamical systems is standard practice, and the standard route is spectral: model the transfer (Koopman) operator by its leading modes. We show that on systems assembled from several weakly interacting components --- a structure common in physical and biological settings --- this may either require an exponential number of modes, or drop an entire component: the component is absent from the model rather than modeled coarsely, and no function of it can be predicted at any accuracy. We call this linear masking.
  The cause is that a rank-based model pays one coordinate per mode. We propose to score instead the $σ$-algebra the coordinates generate, so that products and powers come free and a component's cost is governed only by its generators rather than by all its interactions. The criterion is a $χ^2$-divergence between the embedded present and future, and it carries a budget guarantee: twice the intrinsic dimension of the dynamics is enough coordinates for an embedding whose algebra carries the operator's entire spectrum, with its full infinite rank.
  In variational form the criterion admits off-the-shelf estimators, and restricting its critic to the bilinear class returns the VAMP score on the span, so rank-based methods are one end of the same family. We demonstrate the proposed objective on a composite of published benchmark systems. We exhibit examples where the rank-based methods completely miss the masked components at all ranks $k<100$, while ten algebra coordinates recover all of them. In addition, the resulting algebra representation supports predicting the masked components from few labels, while direct regression from the high-dimensional observation or from the VAMP features fail.

</details>


### [239] [Lost in Aggregation: How Benchmarks Overlook Irreplaceable Model Strengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18919)
*Andrej Tschalzev,Stefan Lüdtke,Heiner Stuckenschmidt,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出应从"数据为中心的峰值性能前沿"视角评估表格机器学习基准，而非仅依赖平均分数、排名等聚合指标。通过TabArena基准分析发现，聚合指标与数据集级别的不可替代性关联较弱，导致"平庸但稳健"的模型被高估，而具有独特数据集优势的模型被低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准评估通过聚合指标（平均分数、排名、成对胜率）衡量模型整体表现，但这些指标回答的是"哪个模型最稳健"，而非"哪些模型对特定数据集达到峰值性能是必要的"。聚合指标可能掩盖模型在特定数据集上的独特价值。

Method: 提出数据中心的峰值性能前沿框架，将模型在前沿上的位置分为四类：不可替代（irreplaceable）、充分（sufficient）、冗余（redundant）、不可靠（fallible）。将该框架应用于TabArena基准，分析聚合指标与数据集级不可替代性之间的关系。

Result: 发现聚合指标之间高度相关，主要衡量模型的一致性和避免失败的能力，但与数据集级不可替代性对齐程度较低。表现尚可但从未达到最佳的模型在聚合指标中获得高评价，而具有独特数据集优势的模型在聚合下显得平庸。

Conclusion: 基准评估进展不仅应关注聚合指标改进，还应衡量新模型是否扩展了各数据集上可达到的峰值性能集合。这为表格学习基准评估提供了新的视角和评价维度。

Abstract: Tabular machine learning benchmarks typically summarize performance by averaging scores, ranks, or pairwise wins across datasets. Such aggregates are useful for selecting robust default models, but they can obscure a different question: which models are necessary to attain peak performance on particular datasets? We argue that benchmark evaluation should also consider the data-centric peak performance frontier, defined by the best statistically supported performance achieved on each dataset. From this perspective, a model may be irreplaceable, sufficient, redundant, or fallible depending on where it lies on the frontier relative to other models. Applying this framework to the TabArena benchmark, we find that common aggregation metrics are highly correlated and largely measure consistency and avoiding failures, while being much less aligned with dataset-level irreplaceability. Consequently, models performing decently across datasets without ever being the best choice are rewarded while models with unique dataset-specific strengths appear mediocre under aggregation. Hence, benchmark progress should be measured not only by improvements on aggregation metrics but also by whether new models expand the set of attainable peak performances across datasets.

</details>


### [240] [Transportable Causal Effect Estimation across Networks under Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18932)
*Xiaojing Du,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Debo Cheng,Jixue Liu,Thuc Duy Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TranCE算法，解决网络干扰下因果效应在不同网络人群间的迁移问题。通过扩展选择图到网络设置，将协变量偏移和结构网络偏移作为独立选择器，推导出直接效应、溢出效应和总效应的迁移公式，并实现双稳健估计与交叉拟合推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络干扰下的因果效应估计假设训练网络与部署网络一致，但实际场景中干预在一个群体上实施而研究问题关注另一个群体，两者在网络拓扑、节点协变量组成和溢出路径上存在差异。这是一个尚无现有算法解决的数据融合问题。

Method: 将选择图扩展到网络设置，使协变量偏移和结构网络偏移作为独立选择器进入模型；基于此推导部署人群中直接效应、溢出效应和总效应的传输公式；进一步实现TranCE算法，结合干预结果模型、域密度比校正和交叉拟合推断，实现双稳健估计。

Result: 在两个基于真实社交网络的半合成基准和一个完全真实的天气保险现场实验中验证了方法有效性，迁移效应与保留的随机化估计结果一致。

Conclusion: 该方法有望改善网络系统（特别是社交网络和公共卫生领域）中的干预策略，填补了跨网络因果效应迁移的空白。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects under network interference typically assumes that the network used for training and the network used for deployment coincide. In practice, an intervention is run on one population while the question of interest concerns a different population, and the two generally differ in topology, node-covariate composition, and spillover pathways. Transporting a causal effect across networks is therefore a data-fusion problem that no existing algorithm solves. We employ a selection diagram, extended to the network setting so that covariate shift and structural network shift enter as separate selectors, and derive from it a transport formula for the direct, spillover, and total effects in the deployment population. Each formula makes explicit which interventional mechanism is assumed invariant and which observational distribution must be reweighted. We then turn the formulas into TranCE (Transported Causal Effects), a doubly-robust algorithm combining an interventional outcome model, a domain density-ratio correction, and cross-fitted inference. Extensive experiments on two semi-synthetic benchmarks derived from real-world social networks and on a fully real weather-insurance field experiment, where the transported effects are checked against held-out randomized estimates, confirm the effectiveness of our approach. Our findings have the potential to improve intervention strategies in networked systems, particularly in social networks and public health.

</details>


### [241] [Training Chemical Plausibility-Aware Large Language Models for Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18940)
*Bogdan Zagribelnyy,Ivan Ilin,Nikita Bondarev,Maksim Kuznetsov,Mathieu Reymond,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Top-K提示范式用于单步逆合成预测，解决其一对多本质问题。作者构建了包含4560万条验证反应的大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL，训练了C3LM（化学约束一致性语言模型），结合ChemCensor和基于新颖性的奖励进行微调，在OOD URSA-expert-2026基准上达到SOTA。分析发现LLM与传统模型探索互补的反应空间，支持集成式逆合成系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步逆合成预测本质上是一对多问题（一个产物对应多种合理反应路径），但现有的评估与基准协议仅关注单一答案，无法充分捕捉这种多样性。现有方法未能有效处理这一根本性挑战。

Method: 1) 提出Top-K提示（Top-K prompting）作为稳健的训练和推理范式；2) 构建超大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL（约4560万条验证反应）；3) 训练C3LM（Chemistry Constraint-Consistent Language Model）；4) 集成ChemCensor（化学约束校验）和新颖性导向的奖励进行微调。

Result: 在OOD URSA-expert-2026基准上达到最先进性能；反应独特性分析表明LLM与传统模型探索互补的反应空间，暗示集成式逆合成系统的潜力。

Conclusion: Top-K、可感知合理性的训练范式为未来基于LLM的稳健合成规划提供了实用的新方向，有望更好地处理逆合成预测的多样性本质。

Abstract: Single-step retrosynthesis is a central component of computer-aided synthesis planning, yet its intrinsically one-to-many nature is poorly captured by single-answer evaluation and benchmarking protocols. To address this, we introduce Top-K prompting as a robust training and inference paradigm to better capture diverse, plausible reaction predictions. We compile CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL, an ultra-large-scale dataset of ~45.6 million verified reactions to train the C3LM (Chemistry Constraint-Consistent Language Model). By integrating fine-tuning with ChemCensor-based and novelty-oriented rewards, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the OOD URSA-expert-2026 benchmark. Further analysis of reaction uniqueness shows that LLMs and conventional models explore complementary reaction spaces, motivating ensemble-based retrosynthesis systems. Overall, our results establish Top-K, plausibility-aware training as a practical new direction for robust future LLM-based synthesis planning.

</details>


### [242] [Fuzzy Accuracy Compensates for Label Subjectivity in Classification of Skin Tone Using Wearable Photoplethysmography Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18969)
*Padmini Krishnadas,Urs Hackstein,Alen Bosnjakovic,Philip J. Aston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用光电容积脉搏波（PPG）信号进行皮肤色调分类（六类Fitzpatrick肤色），由于标签存在主观性误差，传统准确率仅40-55%。通过引入"模糊准确率"（预测类别与标签差异不超过1类即算正确），准确率大幅提升至96%。实验比较了三种机器学习方法：深度学习/树模型处理原始信号、SPAR图像深度学习和特征提取机器学习，其中使用模糊交叉熵损失函数的深度学习在原始信号上效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统皮肤色调分类准确率低（40-55%），但这可能源于标签本身的不可靠性——标签通过人眼对比色卡主观确定，含有大量小尺度误差。因此需要一种更合理的评估方式来验证PPG信号是否真的包含可辨识的皮肤色调信息。

Method: 采用三种方法：(1) 在原始PPG信号上使用深度学习和树模型（深度学习采用模糊交叉熵损失函数）；(2) 使用对称投影吸引子重构（SPAR）方法将信号转换为图像表示，再用深度学习进行分类；(3) 从PPG信号提取特征后进行机器学习分类。评估采用"模糊准确率"指标，允许预测类与标签相差不超过1类。

Result: 模糊交叉熵的深度学习在原始信号上效果最佳；树模型在原始信号上准确率55%、模糊准确率96%；SPAR图像深度学习准确率44%、模糊准确率85%；PPG特征机器学习准确率42%、模糊准确率87%。

Conclusion: 通过模糊准确率评估，证明了PPG信号能以高精度（最多偏差一类）预测皮肤色调，表明PPG信号确实受到皮肤色调的可辨识影响。这为PPG信号中的皮肤色调建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We consider the problem of classification of skin tone using photoplethysmography (PPG) signals with labels of the ordinal six-class Fitzpatrick skin tones. A typical accuracy for this task is a poor 40-55 %. However, the labels are subjectively determined by comparing the skin with a colour chart, and hence contain widespread small-scale inaccuracies. By working with a "fuzzy accuracy", which deems a prediction of skin tone class to be correct if its difference from the labelled class is not greater than one, much higher accuracy is obtained which provides more convincing evidence that skin tone can be accurately predicted from PPG signals. Three machine learning approaches were used, namely deep learning or tree-based approaches on raw PPG signals, deep learning on image representations of the signals generated by the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method, and machine learning on features extracted from the signals. The first method also employed a fuzzy version of the cross entropy loss function, which gave the best results. Tree-based models on raw signals give accuracies up to 55 % and higher fuzzy accuracies up to 96 %, while deep learning models on the SPAR images obtained lower results of 44 % accuracy and 85 % fuzzy accuracy. The machine learning on PPG features gave similar results to the SPAR method with accuracy of 42 % and fuzzy accuracy of 87 %. We have shown that classification of skin tone using PPG signals is possible with high fuzzy accuracy which implies that our modelling approach enables accurate prediction of skin tone class within at most one class of the observer's choice of class, from which we conclude that PPG signals are affected by skin tone in a discernible way.

</details>


### [243] [Monroe: A Molecular Foundation Model for In-Context Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18982)
*Blazej Banaszewski,Andrew W. Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Monroe是一个新型分子基础模型(MFM)，通过在8100万分子上进行预训练，改进立体化学图表示、优化训练损失（构象去噪和嵌入去相关）、改进多任务学习，并引入TabPFN进行下游上下文预测。在Polaris基准上达到或超过现有MFM，在活性悬崖基准上显著优于先前方法，且PFN方法可泛化到其他模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中的生物活性预测常常受限于数据不足，因为湿实验数据生成既耗时又昂贵。现有的分子基础模型旨在通过预训练编码通用化学知识来解决数据受限场景下的泛化问题，但仍存在提升空间。

Method: 1) 扩大预训练规模至8100万分子（PM6量子化学数据集）；2) 改进图表示以更好编码立体化学信息；3) 引入构象去噪和嵌入去相关等改进训练损失；4) 改进多任务学习；5) 使用先验数据拟合模型(TabPFN)进行下游上下文预测。

Result: 在Polaris基准上Monroe匹配或超过现有MFM；在活性悬崖基准上显著优于先前方法。消融和迁移实验表明PFN下游预测器也能提升MiniMol和CheMeleon两个现有模型，产生新的SOTA变体MiniMol_PFN和CheMeleon_PFN。

Conclusion: Monroe通过多种创新（规模、立体化学编码、损失函数、多任务学习、TabPFN下游预测）在数据受限的活性预测场景中取得显著改进，且其下游适应策略（PFN）具有良好泛化性，可迁移到其他MFM上提升性能。

Abstract: Bioassay activity prediction is often data-limited because drug-discovery datasets rely on time-consuming and expensive wet-lab experiments for data generation and evaluation. This challenge has inspired recent research into molecular foundation models (MFMs), which aim to encode general-purpose chemical knowledge into molecular representations that generalize well in data-constrained scenarios. This paper presents Monroe, a new MFM with several innovations over the existing state of the art: increased scale allowing pre-training on over 81 million molecules from the PM6 quantum chemistry dataset; improved graph representation of stereochemistry; improved training losses including conformer denoising and embedding decorrelation; improved multi-task learning; and the use of a prior-data-fitted model (TabPFN) for downstream in-context prediction. Our evaluations use a principled pairwise comparison framework that measures statistically significant performance differences. Across established Polaris benchmarks, Monroe matches or exceeds existing MFMs, while on activity cliff benchmarks, designed to assess utility for molecular discovery, it achieves significant improvements over prior methods. Finally, ablation and transfer experiments show that PFN-based downstream predictors also substantially improve two leading existing models, MiniMol and CheMeleon, yielding new state-of-the-art variants we call MiniMol_PFN and CheMeleon_PFN, suggesting that our downstream adaptation strategy generalizes beyond Monroe. Source code is at github.com/blazejba/monroe.

</details>


### [244] [Harness Continual Learning: Continual Adaptation Beyond Model Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19013)
*Borui Kang,Jinrui Gu,Junhan Lv,Wenbin Li,Lei Wang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的持续学习范式——Harness Continual Learning (HCL)，在冻结基础模型的同时，通过演化代理的外围组件（提示、记忆、工具、技能和路由规则）来实现能力的持续积累，并引入守卫式演化机制来防止harness更新导致的行为遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的持续学习以模型为中心，将模型参数视为随经验变化的状态。但现代智能体还可以通过harness（提示、记忆、工具、技能和路由规则）进行适应，而harness的更新即使在模型冻结时也可能破坏先前可靠的行为。因此需要一个新框架来解决harness层面上的持续学习与遗忘问题。

Method: 提出HCL框架，包含四个执行面向的组件：Task Interface（任务接口）、Experience Memory（经验记忆）、Capability Map（能力图谱）和Adaptive Router（自适应路由器）。引入守卫式harness演化，将更新生成与状态提交分离：Continual Optimizer从执行后反馈中生成候选harness，Continual Evaluator则仅在当前改进、历史保留和有效性检查通过后才提交候选harness。

Result: 在文本推理、多模态感知和开放世界交互等实验上展示了能力积累和失败恢复，在多种设置下相对于基线取得了超过10%的收益。组件消融实验评估了各harness组件的贡献，受控保留扫描揭示了可测量的harness级遗忘，并表明稳定性-可塑性权衡可以被显式调节。

Conclusion: HCL为harness层面的持续学习提供了新的范式，证明了在不修改基础模型的情况下，智能体可以通过精心设计的harness演化机制实现能力的持续提升，同时有效管理harness级遗忘和稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Abstract: Continual learning has largely been model-centric, treating model parameters as the state that changes with sequential experience. Modern agents can also adapt through a harness of prompts, memories, tools, skills, and routing rules. Because these contents jointly shape later execution, a harness update can disrupt previously reliable behavior even when the model is frozen. This raises a new question: how can an agent continually improve its state outside the model while retaining behavior acquired earlier? We formulate Harness Continual Learning (HCL), a new continual learning paradigm in which the harness evolves around a frozen foundation model, and define the resulting loss of earlier behavior as harness-level forgetting. We instantiate HCL with four execution-facing components: the Task Interface, Experience Memory, Capability Map, and Adaptive Router. We further introduce guarded harness evolution to separate update generation from state commitment. A Continual Optimizer proposes candidate harnesses from post-execution feedback, and a Continual Evaluator commits the resulting candidate harness only after checking current improvement, historical retention, and validity. Experiments on textual reasoning, multimodal perception, and open-world interaction demonstrate capability accumulation and failure recovery, with relative gains exceeding 10% over corresponding baselines in multiple settings. Component ablations assess the contribution of each harness component, while controlled retention sweeps reveal measurable harness-level forgetting and show that the stability--plasticity trade-off can be explicitly adjusted.

</details>


### [245] [Multi-Agent Off-Policy Deep Reinforcement Learning for Smart Campus Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19049)
*Omar Rady,Mohamed Ayman,Ali Arafa,Mohamed Shalma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在真实非凸校园拓扑中利用深度强化学习（DRL）优化毫米波基站部署的问题，将基站布置建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并系统对比了四种DRL方案（单智能体DQN、多智能体DQN、单智能体DDPG和多智能体DDPG），结果表明多智能体DDPG在密集场景下表现最优，实现了全覆盖和0.94的公平性Jain指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波基站部署是一个NP-hard的优化问题，传统方法难以处理其非凸、非光滑的max-min公平性目标函数。DRL具有实时适应性和在复杂优化问题中的有效性，因此作者希望探索利用DRL来解决这一具有挑战性的基站部署问题。

Method: 将基站布置问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），系统对比四种深度强化学习方案：离散单智能体DQN、空间划分多智能体DQN、连续单智能体DDPG以及地理划分多智能体DDPG框架，在真实非凸校园拓扑中进行基准测试。

Result: 数值评估表明：在密集场景下多智能体DDPG显著优于单智能体方法；实现了全覆盖目标；获得了0.94的公平性Jain指数；多智能体方法在400个用户的密集场景中展现出高效的收敛性能。

Conclusion: 多智能体DDPG框架是解决毫米波基站部署问题的最优DRL方案，尤其在密集用户场景下具有明显优势，不仅实现了较好的覆盖和公平性，还具备高效的计算收敛能力。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently gained a great attention due to its real-time adaptation and effectiveness in complex optimization problems. This paper investigates the optimal deployment of millimeter-wave (mmWave) base stations (BSs) in a realistic, non-convex campus topology. The optimization problem is NP-hard, due to the non-convex, non-smooth nature of the max-min fairness objective. To overcome these constraints, we formulate the BS placement as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and systematically benchmark four DRL schemes: a discrete single-agent Deep Q-Network (DQN), a spatially partitioned Multi-Agent DQN, a continuous single-agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG), and a geographically partitioned multi-agent DDPG framework. Numerical evaluations reveal that the multi-agent DDPG approach substantially outperforms single-agent in dense scenarios. Additionally full coverage is achieved, and a fairness Jain's index of 0.94 is obtained. Finally, the multi-agent demonstrates highly efficient computational convergence of dense scenarios with $400$ users.

</details>


### [246] [Does Mapping Non-Maximal Probabilities to GMM Components Matter for S-JEPA Encoder Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19084)
*Wenxuan He,Yunpeng Li,Shan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究S-JEPA中软高斯混合模型(GMM)后验概率的作用机制，通过两个匹配对照组(FIXED-RANDPERM和UNIFORM-TAIL)验证：除概率数值本身外，非最大概率值具体分配给哪些GMM分量同样重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-JEPA使用软GMM后验而非硬聚类标签以保留不确定性，但此前尚不清楚仅有概率值是否足够，或者非最大概率分配给哪些GMM分量也会影响学习到的Encoder表示。

Method: 设置两个匹配对照：(1) FIXED-RANDPERM保留top-1分量及概率与非最大概率值的多重集，但按每帧固定的映射重排非最大概率值；(2) UNIFORM-TAIL保留top-1分量、其概率及非最大总质量，但将该质量均匀分配。在三个独立种子、两个冻结Encoder读出任务上进行对比，并进行曝光实验与Phase 2在线GMM轨迹的定性分析。

Result: 真实软目标(REAL SOFT)在两个冻结Encoder读出任务上均优于两个对照组。它更好地恢复了原始GMM尾部，并在控制当前帧完整频谱后，在短时间尺度上获得更大的频谱动力学可及性。在两个曝光实验中，随着更多帧保留原始映射，两种读出均整体改善。

Conclusion: 软目标的数值概率结构不能完全决定学习到的Encoder表示——非最大概率值到GMM分量的映射本身也起作用，这对软目标设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: S-JEPA uses soft Gaussian mixture model (GMM) posteriors instead of hard cluster labels to preserve uncertainty. It remains unclear whether the probability values alone are sufficient, or whether it also matters which GMM components receive the non-maximal probabilities. We test this with two matched controls. FIXED-RANDPERM keeps the top-1 component and probability together with the multiset of non-maximal probability values, but reassigns those non-maximal values using a mapping fixed for each physical frame. UNIFORM-TAIL keeps the top-1 component, its probability, and total non-maximal mass but distributes that mass uniformly. Across three independent seeds, REAL SOFT outperforms both controls on two frozen Encoder readouts. It provides better recovery of the original GMM tail and greater accessibility of spectral dynamics over short time scales after controlling for the complete spectrum of the current frame. In two exposure experiments, both readouts improved overall as more frames retained the original mapping. We also descriptively follow one Phase 2 trajectory after the switch to the online GMM. These results show that the numerical probability structure of the soft target does not fully determine the learned Encoder representation. The mapping of non-maximal probabilities to GMM components also matters.

</details>


### [247] [Open-MOPD: Diagnosing and Fixing Capability Imbalance in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19098)
*Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Yong Yan,Shiyuan Feng,Hanlin Wu,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多教师在线策略蒸馏（M-OPD）在强化学习中的能力整合问题。作者在SmolLM3-3B-Base上建立了受控基准，发现标准M-OPD仅能捕获35.6%的可用性能提升空间（相对于域路由oracle集成）。研究发现失败根源并非梯度冲突，而是token级优化预算的严重错配，由三个因素驱动：序列长度差异、非均匀学习率导致的收敛漂移、异步策略更新导致的多步奖励陈旧性。作者提出Open-MOPD框架，包含token份额平衡、差距感知动态预算分配和学生奖励刷新三个机制，将headroom恢复率从35.6%提升至83.4%，并完全开源所有代码和评估套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师蒸馏虽然实践上成功，但其优化动力学机制尚不明确，缺乏严格可复现的配方，需要系统理解能力整合的失效机制。

Method: 建立受控M-OPD基准（SmolLM3-3B-Base + oracle路由），诊断出三个导致token优化预算错配的因素，提出Open-MOPD框架包含token份额平衡、差距感知动态预算分配和学生奖励刷新三个机制。

Result: 标准M-OPD仅恢复35.6%的headroom，而Open-MOPD将恢复率提升至83.4%，在单一可部署学生模型中系统性地恢复了跨域平衡，并在学术可负担的硬件预算下开源了完整的后训练配方。

Conclusion: M-OPD失败源于token级优化预算分配不当而非梯度冲突，通过系统性的预算平衡机制可以大幅提升多教师能力整合效率，且该配方是开放、可复现的。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (M-OPD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for consolidating domain-specialized reinforcement learning (RL) experts into a single generalist student via dense, token-level reward supervision. Despite its practical success, the optimization dynamics governing multi-teacher capability integration remain poorly understood, and open, rigorously reproducible recipes are conspicuously lacking. In this work, we establish a controlled M-OPD benchmark on SmolLM3-3B-Base with oracle routing, isolating capability integration from routing ambiguity. Our investigation reveals a pronounced capability integration gap: standard M-OPD captures only 35.6% of the available headroom relative to a domain-routed oracle ensemble, with concise tasks such as instruction following suffering severe degradation and premature stagnation. Crucially, we show that this failure stems not from gradient conflict, but from a severe misallocation of the token-level optimization budget. This pathology is driven by three orthogonal factors: structural sequence-length disparities across domains, dynamic convergence drift due to non-uniform learning rates, and multi-step reward staleness from asynchronous policy updates. To resolve these imbalances, we introduce Open-MOPD, a principled framework incorporating token-share balancing, gap-aware dynamic budget allocation, and student reward refresh. Together, these mechanisms systematically restore cross-domain balance, elevating headroom recovery from 35.6% to 83.4% in a single deployable student. We fully open-source our end-to-end post-training recipe, training trajectories, and evaluation suites on an academically accessible hardware budget.

</details>


### [248] [Enhancing EBSD throughput of battery electrode materials using super-resolution generative adversarial networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19117)
*John Mangum,Andrew Glaws,Francois Usseglio-Viretta,Steven Spurgeon,Donal Finegan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成对抗网络（SRGAN）的机器学习超分辨率框架，用于显著提升锂离子电池电极材料（NMC正极）的EBSD表征效率。通过将低分辨率EBSD数据增强至高分辨率，与经典插值方法相比在2x-12x放大倍数下均表现更优，并证明5x放大（相当于25倍采集速度提升）在实际应用中保持可接受的微观结构精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBSD是优化锂电池电极材料性能的关键定量微观结构表征方法，但其采集速度本质上较慢，阻碍了获得统计代表性微观结构数据所需的高通量分析。

Method: 采用SRGAN（基于生成对抗网络的超分辨率模型），在LiNixMnyCozO2（NMC）正极颗粒的EBSD数据上训练，将低分辨率数据集计算增强为高分辨率，并对照经典插值方法在不同放大倍数（2x至12x）下进行性能比较。

Result: SRGAN在定性图像指标和定量微观结构分析上系统性地优于经典方法，尤其在保留小晶粒和维持真实晶界方面表现突出。在5x放大倍数下，晶粒面积等效直径、晶粒最大球内接直径和晶界长度的相对误差分别为+5.7%、+8.2%和-14.6%，同时获得25倍的采集速度提升或25倍的视场扩大。

Conclusion: SRGAN方法显著提升了EBSD采集效率，使EBSD能够作为高通量表征工具应用于材料研究和工业过程开发，为获得统计上更稳健的微观结构数据集提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Quantitative microstructural characterization of Li-ion battery electrode materials using electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) has been proven as a critical method for optimizing cell performance. However, the inherently slow nature of EBSD can hinder the throughput of analyses needed for statistical representation of a material microstructure being developed. This work demonstrates a machine learning super-resolution framework using a generative adversarial network (SRGAN) to significantly increase EBSD throughput. The SRGAN model was trained on EBSD data of LiNixMnyCozO2 (NMC) cathode particles to computationally enhance low-resolution datasets and its performance is compared against classical interpolation methods across various upscaling factors (2x to 12x). Both qualitative image metrics and quantitative microstructural analysis verified that the SRGAN systematically outperformed classical methods, particularly in preserving small grains and maintaining realistic grain boundaries. We demonstrate that a 5x upscaling factor, corresponding to a 25x speed-up in acquisition time or a 25x larger field of view, is practical while maintaining acceptable accuracy in key metrics like grain size and shape. For instance, at 5x upscaling, relative errors were +5.7%, +8.2%, and -14.6% on grain area-equivalent diameter, grain maximum sphere-inscribed diameter, and grain boundary length, respectively. The SRGAN methodology developed in this work significantly enhances the efficiency of EBSD acquisition for more statistically robust microstructural dataset, enabling EBSD as a high-throughput characterization tool for materials research and industrial process development.

</details>


### [249] [Discretizing Continuous Time Series for Imputation with Masked Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19119)
*Dongbin Kim,Seungyun Lee,Geonwoo Shin,Jaewook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MDTIM模型，利用掩码扩散模型的训练范式进行时间序列插补。通过将MASK token与有效观测在表示空间中正交分离，并直接预测原始值而非噪声，同时引入随机离散化机制将连续值映射为有序感知的token，从而在多种缺失场景下超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列插补方法存在两个局限：1）缺失值与观测值被嵌入同一表示空间，缺乏显式结构分离；2）基于连续扩散的方法被训练为预测添加的噪声而非原始信号，与插补任务目标不一致。

Method: 1) 提出MASK token，与有效观测在表示空间中结构正交；2) 模型直接预测原始值而非噪声，使学习目标与插补任务自然对齐；3) 引入Stochastic Discretization机制，将连续时间序列值映射为有序感知（ordinal-aware）的离散token，同时保留连续动态特性，从而弥合离散掩码扩散与连续时间序列之间的差距。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，MDTIM在多样的缺失场景下展现出优越的鲁棒性和可扩展性，持续优于现有的确定性及生成式最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将掩码扩散模型训练范式适配到时间序列插补任务，并在表示空间和学习目标两个层面与任务对齐，MDTIM有效解决了现有方法的固有缺陷，验证了该范式在时间序列插补中的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Time series imputation is a crucial area for reliable time series analysis, yet it remains challenging due to the complex temporal dynamics and noise of real-world data. Existing approaches, however, exhibit two limitations: missing and observed values are embedded within the same representation space without explicit structural separation, and continuous diffusion-based methods are trained to predict added noise rather than the original signal. To address these, we propose the Masked Diffusion Time-series Imputation Model (MDTIM), which leverages the training paradigm of masked diffusion model for imputation tasks. The MASK token is structurally orthogonal to valid observations, and the model directly predicts the original values, naturally aligning both the representation and the learning objective with the imputation task. To bridge the gap between discrete masked diffusion and the continuous, ordinal nature of time series, we further introduce Stochastic Discretization, which maps continuous values to ordinal-aware tokens while preserving continuous dynamics. Our experiments on diverse benchmarks confirm that MDTIM achieves superior robustness and scalability, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art deterministic and generative baselines across various missing scenarios.

</details>


### [250] [PGFS++: Molecular Property Improvement under Synthesis and Diversity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19121)
*Boqiao Zhang,Godbless James,Sai Krishna Gottipati,Andrew Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PGFS++，一种合成感知的强化学习框架，用于针对特定输入分子的分子改进任务，在提升目标性质的同时保持输出多样性并生成显式的合成路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期药物发现中分子优化任务频繁出现，但无约束化学空间中优化的分子往往难以合成，实用性有限。PGFS虽然考虑了合成可行性，但其反应物嵌入预测方式导致反应物选择间接，限制了学习效果。此外，更强大的反应物搜索会引发奖励黑客问题，导致输出多样性崩溃。

Method: 首先开发PGFS+，使用可训练嵌入查找表表示反应模板和第二反应物，并结合更有效的评分函数和强化学习算法。随后引入PGFS++，将输入分子视为前向合成轨迹的起点，应用学习到的反应模板与库存中的构建单元，生成具有改进性质、显式合成路线且与输入结构相似的分子。

Result: 在分子改进任务上的实验表明，PGFS++在提升目标性质的同时保持了较高的输出多样性，避免了对同一高奖励分子的输出崩溃。

Conclusion: PGFS++通过输入特定的前向合成轨迹生成分子，有效解决了分子改进任务中可合成性、性质提升和输出多样性之间的权衡问题，为合成感知的分子优化提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Improving molecular properties, such as drug-likeness or binding affinity, is a recurring task in early-stage drug discovery. However, molecules optimized in an unconstrained chemical space have limited practical value if they cannot be synthesized. Policy Gradient for Forward Synthesis (PGFS) is a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning method for molecular improvement, but its use of reactant embedding prediction makes reactant selection indirect, which, as we show, limits learning effectiveness. We first develop PGFS+, in which reaction templates and second reactants are represented by trainable embedding lookup tables. Combined with a more effective scoring function and RL algorithm, PGFS+ significantly improves the desired property. However, it exposes a reward-hacking failure mode: a powerful reactant search can map diverse input molecules to the same high-reward magnet molecule, improving the reward while collapsing the output diversity. We therefore introduce PGFS++, a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning framework for input-specific molecular improvement. Given an input molecule, PGFS++ treats it as the start of a forward-synthesis trajectory, applies learned reaction templates with compatible in-stock building blocks, and produces a molecule with improved target properties, an explicit synthesis route, and structural similarity to the input. Experiments on molecular improvement tasks show that PGFS++ improves target properties while preserving high output diversity.

</details>


### [251] [Leaf Values as Coordinates: Exact Contrastive Explanation for Gradient-Boosted Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19127)
*Emanuele Luzio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将梯度提升集成模型中每棵树的叶子值视为坐标而非中间结果，从而使每个实例成为R^M空间中的一个点，模型以线性方式作用于该点。这种视角转变使得对比解释变得精确：两个实例的差异向量在共享叶子处为零，拒绝与接受申请人之间的差距可由少数可追溯的坐标承载。基于此表示构建了反事实解释（recourse）方法，在五个表格数据集上验证，其重建模型决策精度达6.2×10^-15，且在可行性约束下保留58%有效性（最强基线为41%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对梯度提升树的对比解释通常依赖拟合、采样或对特征可加性的假设，这些方法不够精确且无法保证解释忠实于模型本身。作者希望通过改变视角——将叶子值视为坐标——获得精确、可审计的对比解释，无需任何近似。

Method: 将梯度提升集成模型重新解释为：每个实例在多维空间R^M中是一个点（M为树的数量），模型的分数是该点坐标之和。两个实例的差异由向量表示，该向量仅在它们落入不同叶子的坐标上非零，每个非零坐标可追溯到具体的树和具体的分裂。基于此表示构建反事实解释方法，仅推荐实例可实际执行的修改。

Result: 在五个表格数据集上通过重复交叉验证评估。推荐结果以6.2×10^-15的精度重建模型自身决策，审计者无需模型即可复核算术。在信用数据集上与努力程度和现实性的帕累托前沿上不被支配。当推荐限制为可实际执行的变化（排除年龄、已结清拖欠等不可变特征）时，保留了58%有效性，而最强基线仅保留41%。

Conclusion: 通过将叶子值重新解释为坐标，可以在不引入任何近似假设的情况下获得精确的梯度提升模型对比解释。这种表示天然支持可执行的反事实推荐，且标准评估方法因未考虑推荐是否可实际执行而掩盖了本方法的真实优势。

Abstract: A gradient-boosted ensemble predicts by summing one leaf value per tree. Read
  those values as coordinates rather than as intermediate results, and every
  instance becomes a point in R^M on which the model acts linearly: the score is
  the sum of the coordinates.
  This small change of view makes contrastive explanation exact. The difference
  between two instances is a vector that is identically zero wherever they share
  a leaf, so the gap between a rejected applicant and an accepted one is carried
  by a handful of coordinates, each traceable to a real split in a real tree.
  Nothing is fitted, sampled, or assumed additive in features -- the additivity
  is already there, in the right space.
  We build a recourse method on this representation and evaluate it on five
  tabular datasets under repeated cross-validation. Its recommendation
  reconstructs the model's own decision to 6.2 x 10^-15, so an auditor can
  re-check the arithmetic without the model. On the credit datasets it is
  Pareto-non-dominated on effort against realism. And when recommendations are
  restricted to changes the subject could actually make -- not their age, not a
  settled delinquency -- it retains 58% of its validity where the strongest
  baseline retains 41%, a distinction the standard evaluation cannot see because
  it never asks whether a recommendation can be carried out.

</details>


### [252] [Beyond Trial Averaging: Anchoring Neural and Visual Representations for Few-Repetition Brain-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19128)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEAR框架，通过将高重复次数中心作为锚点，从两侧逼近——去噪器将噪声查询拉向真实锚点，小网络预测图像的伪锚点并将图像拉向其——从而显著提升少重复次数条件下的脑信号到图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑信号到图像检索方法通常需要平均多次（最多80次）神经试验才能获得好效果，但少重复次数（1-4次）时性能急剧下降。此前被归因于查询噪声，但作者发现非传递性对齐模式表明问题不仅是查询噪声，图库（gallery）放置位置也影响检索性能。

Method: 提出NEAR框架：1）将高重复次数中心作为锚点；2）双路径逼近——去噪器将低重复噪声查询拉向真实锚点；3）一个小网络从图像预测伪锚点并将图像表示拉向该锚点。通过这种双侧对齐策略减少对重复采集的依赖。

Result: 在四个数据集（涵盖EEG、MEG和fMRI）上，NEAR在少重复次数场景中一致提升检索性能。在THINGS-EEG2上，平均1次和4次重复时分别将200-way Top-1准确率提升了5.7和9.3个百分点。

Conclusion: NEAR通过锚定神经和视觉表示，显著降低了对重复信号采集的依赖，使脑信号到图像检索更接近实际部署应用。

Abstract: Decoding visual information from brain signals probes neural representations and enables neuro-rehabilitation and dream decoding. Recent brain-to-image retrieval approaches have achieved promising performance, typically by averaging many (up to 80) neural trials per image, requiring repeated stimulus presentation that increases latency, cost, and user burden. When only one or a few repetitions are available, the retrieval accuracy drops sharply. This drop is commonly attributed to query noise because averaging suppresses noise and increases signal stability. However, we find a non-transitive alignment pattern: the low-repetition query signal and the image representation each align with the high-repetition center, but not directly with each other. This pattern shows that query noise is only part of the problem and that gallery placement also affects retrieval. We therefore propose a neural-anchor-based retrieval (NEAR) framework that treats the high-repetition center as an anchor and approaches it from both sides: a denoiser pulls the noisy query toward the true anchor, and a small network predicts each candidate's pseudo anchor from its image and pulls the image toward it. Across four datasets spanning EEG, MEG and fMRI, NEAR consistently improved retrieval in the few-repetition regime. On THINGS-EEG2, it improved 200-way Top-1 accuracy by 5.7 and 9.3 percentage points respectively, when averaging one and four repetitions. By anchoring neural and visual representations, NEAR reduces reliance on repeated acquisition and brings neural retrieval closer to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [253] [SCORE: Subject Coordinate Recovery for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19134)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Siyang Li,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCORE框架，解决跨受试者EEG-to-image检索中无标签校准的性能差距问题。通过恢复感知的训练与坐标对齐，在200-way检索任务中大幅超越现有基线，无需目标标签或编码器更新即可实现跨用户部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EEG-to-image检索方法在无标签校准的新用户上性能远低于受试者内方法，严重制约实际部署。作者通过跨受试者EEG特征分析发现，不同受试者间概念关系相似但坐标方向不同，这是导致跨受试者性能差距的根本原因。

Method: 提出主题坐标恢复(SCORE)框架，包含两部分：1) 训练阶段：将源受试者EEG与公共图像空间对齐，并通过源只回放模拟未见受试者恢复；2) 部署阶段：冻结双编码器，通过hubness校正匹配选择可靠EEG-图像地标，估计正交变换恢复目标受试者EEG坐标，无需源数据或目标标签。

Result: 在两个公开基准的200-way检索中，SCORE对所有目标受试者均优于未适应基线。在THINGS-EEG2上达到53.23%/83.55%的Top-1/Top-5准确率，在Alljoined-1.6M上达到12.01%/32.16%，分别超过最强基线17.45/15.70和3.08/4.62个百分点。

Conclusion: SCORE通过跨受试者坐标恢复使脑电视觉解码无需目标标签或编码器更新即可实现稳健、实用、低延迟的跨用户部署，显著推动了脑机接口在实际场景中的应用前景。

Abstract: Accurate visual decoding can reveal how the brain represents visual information and recover perceived content from neural signals such as electroencephalography (EEG), with potential for neural communication. However, current EEG-to-image retrieval methods perform far below their within-subject counterparts for new users without labeled calibration, limiting real-world deployment. To understand this gap, we analyze EEG features across subjects and find that different subjects preserve similar relationships among concepts but express them along different coordinate directions. We therefore propose Subject Coordinate Recovery (SCORE), a target label-free framework combining recovery-aware source training with coordinate alignment at deployment. During training, SCORE aligns source subject EEG with a common image space and simulates unseen-subject recovery through source-only episodes. At deployment, with both encoders frozen, SCORE selects reliable EEG-image landmarks through hubness-corrected matching and estimates an orthogonal transformation to recover target EEG coordinates without source data or target labels. In 200-way retrieval on two public benchmarks, SCORE outperforms the unadapted baseline for every target subject and achieves the best overall accuracy. It reaches 53.23%/83.55% and 12.01%/32.16% Top-1/Top-5 on THINGS-EEG2 and Alljoined-1.6M, respectively, surpassing the strongest baselines by 17.45/15.70 and 3.08/4.62 percentage points. Without target labels or encoder updates, SCORE brings brain-based visual decoding closer to robust, practical, low-latency deployment across users.

</details>


### [254] [Bridge Graphical Models: Coupling, Projection, and Current-Preserving Dynamics for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19144)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"马尔可夫化差距"(Markovization gap)概念，用于量化连续时间生成模型中从端点条件桥到非预期马尔可夫解码器之间的信息损失，并引入桥图模型(BGM)框架来统一比较不同生成模型的这一瓶颈。实验表明，该差距可作为训练前的诊断工具，预测下游训练损失和FID排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成模型通常由端点条件桥构建，但实际生成需要非预期马尔可夫解码器，只观察当前状态和时间。这种从桥到解码器的压缩是扩散模型、流匹配、整流流、Schrödinger桥和基于场的生成模型共同面临的结构性瓶颈，但此前缺乏统一的量化框架来衡量这一不可约的信息损失。

Method: 1) 定义马尔可夫化差距：桥速度在给定马尔可夫状态下的时间积分条件方差，即从采样器可用信息预测端点条件运动的MMSE。2) 提出桥图模型(BGM)，将端点耦合、桥定律、马尔可夫投影和保流动力学表示分离为独立设计选择，使瓶颈在不同模型族间可比较。3) BGM还可表示泊松模型和静电模型作为场线桥核，具有对应的场线马尔可夫化差距。4) 在合成、潜空间和像素空间上进行验证，使用特征空间代理差距在训练前（数分钟内）估计设计选择的排名。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST上的合成、潜空间和像素空间实验中，训练前估算的特征空间代理差距在固定架构、桥、采样器和计算条件下，与下游训练损失和FID的方向一致地排序设计选择。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫化差距可作为桥和耦合设计的训练前诊断工具，在训练之前就评估不同生成模型设计的优劣，节省大量计算资源。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative models are often built from endpoint-conditioned bridges, but generation requires a different object: a non-anticipative Markov decoder that only observes the current state and time. We identify this bridge-to-decoder compression as a structural bottleneck shared by diffusion models, flow matching, rectified flow, Schrödinger bridges, and field-based generative models. We introduce the \emph{Markovization gap}, the time-integrated conditional variance of the bridge velocity given the Markov state. It is the MMSE of predicting endpoint-conditioned motion from the information available to a sampler, and it measures an irreducible loss incurred before any neural network is trained. To make this bottleneck comparable across model families, we define \emph{Bridge Graphical Models} (BGMs), which separate endpoint coupling, bridge law, Markovian projection, and current-preserving dynamics representation as independent design choices. The same formalism also represents Poisson and electrostatic models as field-line bridge kernels with a corresponding field-line Markovization gap. Across synthetic, latent, and pixel-space pilots on CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, a feature-space proxy gap estimated in minutes before training ranks design choices in the same direction as downstream training loss and FID under fixed architecture, bridge, sampler, and compute. These results support the Markovization gap as a pre-training diagnostic for bridge and coupling design.

</details>


### [255] [Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning for Controlled Hawkes Jump-Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19151)
*Tomasz R. Bielecki,Thibaut Mastrolia,Haoze Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多变量Hawkes驱动的随机微分方程在非马尔可夫设定下的随机控制问题，提出了一种有限维马尔可夫化方法将Hawkes过程用混合指数核近似，并在此基础上开发了连续时间确定性策略梯度算法（Hawkes-CT DDPG），通过仅观测事件时间、SDE解和衰减滤波器实现无模型学习，并与离散时间强化学习方法在多种核类型下进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的随机控制理论主要适用于马尔可夫设定，而Hawkes过程的记忆依赖（路径依赖）特性使其超出经典随机控制框架，除非使用特定的马尔可夫核。因此需要新的方法来处理这类非马尔可夫问题。

Method: 1) 开发有限维马尔可夫化程序，用混合指数核近似多变量Hawkes过程，并证明其收敛性；2) 在马尔可夫化近似上提出连续时间确定性策略梯度算法（Hawkes-CT DDPG）；3) 设计无模型算法，仅依赖事件时间、SDE解和衰减滤波器，无需知道Hawkes核系数。

Result: 证明了Hawkes过程、其强度及问题值在马尔可夫化近似下收敛于原始非马尔可夫过程及原问题值；在三种核类型（简单指数、Erlang、幂律）下将Hawkes-CT DDPG与离散时间强化学习方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 所提出的Hawkes-CT DDPG方法能够有效解决非马尔可夫设定下由Hawkes过程驱动的随机控制问题，且在多种核类型下优于或可比于离散时间强化学习方法。

Abstract: We study stochastic control of multivariate Hawkes-driven stochastic differential equations with machine learning algorithms in a non-Markovian setting. Due to the path dependence of the memory of the Hawkes intensity, this problem does not fall within classical stochastic control theory outside particular Markovian kernels. We first develop a finite-dimensional Markovianization procedure and algorithm to approximate multivariate Hawkes processes with mixtures of exponential kernels. We prove the convergence of the Markovianized approximation of the Hawkes process, its intensity, and the value of the problem to the original non-Markovian processes and the value of the primal problem. We then formulate continuous-time deterministic policy gradient learning on the Markovianized approximation of the problem, called Hawkes-CT DDPG. We propose a model-free algorithm to solve the non-Markovian Hawkes-driven optimization by observing only the event times of the process, the realization of the solution to the SDE, and a chosen set of decay filters, while the Hawkes kernel coefficients remain unknown. We compare our continuous time reinforcement learning Hawkes-CT DDPG method with discrete time reinforcement learning techniques under three different types of kernels: simple exponential, Erlang, and power-law kernels.

</details>


### [256] [Learned, Then Lost: A Measured Single-Example Counterfactual in Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19168)
*Zachary Speck,Asa Shepard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在124M参数的GPT-2模型上进行了24次反事实实验（在训练过程中注入单个训练样本），测量单个训练样本对最终模型的贡献。结果显示，注入的段落会在50步后被记住（8/8种子显著），但在训练结束时差异消失。权重位移达到种子间欧氏距离的44.1%，而插值障碍仅为种子间障碍的3.0%，相差约15倍，表明注入将模型在其盆内重新定位而未移出盆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个训练样本对最终模型的贡献通常只能估计而无法测量，因为真正的测量需要两次昂贵且仅在单个批处理行上不同的完整预训练。作者旨在通过小型规模的24次反事实实验来直接测量这种贡献。

Method: 训练了32个124M参数的GPT-2模型（4种条件×8个种子），在训练第200步（共9536步）时，将256行批次中的一行替换为携带194-token段落的固定上下文注入。三种注入条件：流畅散文+真实主体、流畅散文+伪造主体（与真实条件在全批次梯度delta上匹配在0.14%内）、随机键盘字符；第四种为无注入孪生对照。

Result: 注入后50步，看到段落的模型预测段落表现优于未注入模型0.039和0.044 nats（8/8种子，p<10^-4）。但最终步骤无显著差异（p=0.25和p=0.71）。插值障碍对比+0.0068（p=0.509），持出交叉熵-0.00044（p=0.310）。权重位移达种子间距的44.1%，92%在训练中途已稳定，而障碍仅达3.0%。

Conclusion: 单个训练样本被学习后随时间衰减，最终不可检测。注入将模型在其损失景观盆内重新定位而不将其移出盆，因为权重位移与障碍之间存在约15倍的差距（且为下界）。

Abstract: A single training example's contribution to a finished model is normally estimated rather than measured, because measuring it takes two expensive full pre-training runs that differ in one row of one batch. We ran that counterfactual 24 times at a small scale. We trained 32 GPT-2 models at 124M parameters from scratch on OpenWebText, over four conditions and eight seeds. At step 200 of 9,536, at peak learning rate, we replaced one row of a 256-row batch with a fixed context injection carrying a 194-token passage. The three injected conditions are: 1. fluent prose with a corpus-attested subject, 2. fluent prose with a fabricated subject matched to it within 0.14% on full-batch gradient delta, and 3. random keyboard characters. The fourth condition is an uninjected twin. The passage is learned from one exposure and then decays. Fifty steps after injection, the arm that saw a passage predicts it better than the arm that did not by 0.039 and 0.044 nats of cross-entropy on the passage, at eight of eight seeds with p < $10^{-4}$. At the final step we do not detect that difference for either passage, at p = 0.25 and p = 0.71, against minimum detectable effects of 0.025 and 0.079 nats, nor between the two passages, at p=0.54. Every geometric measure we report is taken after that decay. Our pre-registered contrast on interpolation loss barrier is +0.0068 with p = 0.509, against a minimum detectable effect of 0.032 barrier units. Held-out cross-entropy is $-0.00044$ with p = 0.310. Per-layer centered kernel alignment does not detectably separate any condition at any layer. Weight displacement reaches 44.1% of the seed-to-seed Euclidean distance and is 92% settled by the midpoint of training, while the barrier reaches 3.0% of the seed-to-seed barrier. Those two figures sit roughly 15 times apart, and that is a lower bound. The injection relocates the model within its basin without moving it out.

</details>


### [257] [Lévy Attention: Single-Pass Predictive Uncertainty for Continuous-Time Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19171)
*Sotirios P. Chatzis,Loukas Papadoulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lévy Attention，一种基于Lévy过程的跨注意力算子，用于不规则采样时间序列。它通过泊松随机测度将注意力输出的方差以闭式形式计算出来，从而在无需额外训练头的情况下，让每个预测同时报告其可信度（证据质量与值分歧的组合）。该方法在几乎不损失精度的前提下，提供了免费的置信区间信号，性能优于20次MC Dropout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度模型可对不规则采样时间序列在任意连续时间戳回答查询，但无法给出每个答案的置信度。作者希望让注意力层本身同时产生预测与不确定性估计，而不需要额外的网络头或多次采样。

Method: 引入Lévy Attention：将交叉注意力建模为对非齐次泊松随机测度的随机积分。查询-键兼容性在连续(时间×通道)索引空间上构成强度函数，泊松测度在该强度下散布原子，输出对插值后的值域在这些原子上取平均。在期望上等价于软化余弦核注意力，可替代softmax层并以精确梯度训练。通过精确方差恒等式，闭式得到RMS偏差σ̂(q)=√(tr Σ_V(q)·φ(Λ_q))，其中Λ_q为证据质量（总兼容质量），tr Σ_V(q)为值分歧（值扩展）。

Result: 实证显示分歧项携带主要信号，而证据因子的信息量随数据稀疏度变化（密集数据上无信息，稀疏数据上强信息）。在t-PatchGNN上，算子替换代价最多5.6%准确率（稀疏数据上无损）。免费分歧信号在匹配的五种子套件上优于20次MC Dropout；σ̂缩放的校准高斯在零样本CRPS上优于50次采样器；split-conformal包装器在所有置信水平达到名义覆盖率；单次遍历在1.4秒内对3,383个未见患者按可信度排序。

Conclusion: 注意力层本身的随机性结构可以自然产生闭式的预测不确定性，无需额外成本或训练头。Lévy Attention提供了准确率与不确定性估计之间的良好权衡，是一个即插即用的快速不确定性估计方案。

Abstract: Deep models for irregularly-sampled time series answer queries at arbitrary continuous timestamps, yet report nothing about how far each answer should be trusted. We show the attention layer itself can close that gap: with the right stochastic formulation, the pass that makes each prediction also reports, in closed form and at no extra cost, how far it should be trusted. We introduce Lévy Attention, a cross-attention operator whose output is a stochastic integral against an inhomogeneous Poisson random measure: query-key compatibilities assemble an intensity over a continuous (time x channel) index space, the measure scatters atoms under it, and the output averages an interpolated value field at those atoms. In expectation it reduces to a mollified cosine-kernel attention, so it replaces a softmax layer and trains with exact gradients.
  What softmax discards, the Poisson construction preserves in closed form: the evidence $Λ_q$ (total compatibility mass) and the disagreement $\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)$ (value spread). An exact variance identity makes their combination $\hatσ(q)=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)\,\varphi(Λ_q)}$ the root-mean-square deviation of the sampled operator, emitted by the deterministic pass with no trained head.
  Empirically, disagreement carries the signal, while the evidence factor swings from uninformative on dense data to strongly informative on sparse. On t-PatchGNN the operator swap costs at most 5.6% accuracy against a matched control and nothing on the sparsest dataset. The free disagreement signal improves on 20-pass MC dropout across matched five-seed suites, and $\hatσ$ scales a calibrated Gaussian whose zero-sample CRPS beats a fifty-draw sampler; a split-conformal wrapper reaches nominal coverage at every level, and one pass ranks 3,383 unseen patients by trust in 1.4 seconds.

</details>


### [258] [Beyond Teacher Likelihood: Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19181)
*Zhu Zhang,Jixun Wang,Xiaoang Xu,Xiaorong Wang,Zihan Zhou,Zhiyuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Chaojun Xiao,Yuezhi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分组校准在线蒸馏（GC-OPD）方法，通过分离归一化验证器奖励与轨迹级OPD分数，计算带符号的师生不一致残差，并用相对优势信用分配将残差分配到token级别，从而在长上下文任务中提升蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文任务中，传统OPD的token级教师指导可能偏向局部合理但遗漏分布证据或违反全局约束的响应，而任务验证器能在响应级评估任务完成度并返回部分成功的分级奖励。作者诊断发现随着输入范围变长，轨迹级OPD分数与验证器奖励越来越不一致。

Method: GC-OPD在每组rollout内分别归一化验证器奖励和轨迹级OPD分数，将两者之差作为带符号的师生不一致残差；相对优势信用分配（RACA）根据token的相对OPD优势将该残差分配到各token，同时保留原始OPD信号。

Result: 在五个长上下文基准上，GC-OPD将Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B的平均分从29.08提到40.47、从35.12提到44.65，优于vanilla OPD的39.31和43.56。消融实验表明带符号残差优于额外OPD项或直接分组归一化验证器奖励加法，RACA优于均匀token分配。

Conclusion: 分组相对残差校准能在不丢弃密集token级指导的情况下融入验证器结果，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using dense token-level guidance from a stronger teacher. In long-context tasks, however, token-level teacher support can favor locally plausible responses that omit evidence distributed across the input or violate global task constraints. Task-specific verifiers, in contrast, evaluate task completion at the response level and may return graded rewards that reflect partial success. We diagnose this mismatch on fixed responses from two representative long-context evidence-aggregation tasks. Across longer input ranges, trajectory-level OPD scores become progressively less aligned with verifier rewards, indicating teacher-verifier disagreement. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD). GC-OPD separately normalizes verifier rewards and trajectory-level OPD scores within each rollout group and uses their difference as a signed teacher-verifier disagreement residual. Relative-advantage-based credit assignment (RACA) distributes this trajectory-level residual across tokens according to their relative OPD advantages while preserving the original OPD signal. Across five long-context benchmarks, post-training with GC-OPD raises the five-benchmark averages of the official Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B checkpoints from 29.08 to 40.47 and from 35.12 to 44.65, respectively. Vanilla OPD reaches 39.31 and 43.56 under the same setup. Controlled ablations show that the signed residual is more effective than either an additional OPD-derived term or direct group-normalized verifier reward addition, while RACA further improves over uniform token allocation. Together, these results demonstrate that group-relative residual calibration can incorporate verifier outcomes without discarding dense token-level guidance. Code is available at https://github.com/SolereZhang/GC-OPD.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [Low-Power, Neuromorphic, Acoustic Anomaly Detection for Persistent Machine Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18341)
*Steven C. Nesbit,Victor M. Vergara,Michael A. Felix,Evan T. Kain,Luis R. García Carrillo,Gerd J. Kunde,Andrew T. Sornborger*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文展示了在Intel Loihi 2神经形态处理器上实现基于自编码器的声学异常检测系统。在干净的ToyADMOS ToyCar基准上达到0.9959 AUC，在DCASE 2026 Task 2嘈杂基准上超过基线指标，且每样本动态能耗仅0.0406-0.0426 mJ，比CPU和GPU低两个数量级，证明了神经形态计算在低功耗持续机器监控中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续的声学监控可以在无物理接触的情况下检测机器故障，但传统always-on推理模式受限于功耗、延迟和部署复杂性。需要一种低功耗、低延迟的持续监控方案来解决这些问题。

Method: 采用自编码器进行声学异常检测，在Intel Loihi 2神经形态处理器上部署。Log-mel特征在片外计算，而归一化、自编码器推理、L1重建评分和阈值判定均在芯片上运行。在16芯片Loihi 2 VPX系统上进行功耗分析。

Result: 在干净的ToyADMOS ToyCar基准上达到0.9959 AUC和0.9785标准化pAUC；在DCASE 2026 Task 2 ToyCar嘈杂基准上，源域AUC为0.7990、目标域AUC为0.6466、pAUC为0.6426，均超过基线。动态能耗为每样本0.0406-0.0426 mJ，比CPU和GPU低两个数量级，保持实时吞吐。

Conclusion: 神经形态声学异常检测是实现低功耗、持续机器监控的实际可行方案，在性能和能效之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Persistent acoustic monitoring can detect machine faults without physical contact, but always-on inference is constrained by power, latency, and deployment complexity. We demonstrate autoencoder-based acoustic anomaly detection on an Intel Loihi 2 neuromorphic processor under clean and noisy conditions. Log-mel features are computed off chip; normalization, autoencoder inference, L1 reconstruction scoring, and thresholding run on chip. In a clean, microphone-position-invariant ToyADMOS ToyCar benchmark, the on-chip model achieves 0.9959 AUC and 0.9785 standardized pAUC at maximum false-positive rate 0.1. In the DCASE 2026 Task 2 ToyCar noisy benchmark, the model achieves source AUC 0.7990, target AUC 0.6466, and pAUC 0.6426, exceeding reported baseline metrics. Power profiling on a 16-chip Loihi 2 VPX system shows real-time throughput with 0.0406$\unicode{x2013}$0.0426 mJ dynamic energy per sample, two orders of magnitude lower than both a CPU and GPU. These results support neuromorphic acoustic anomaly detection as a practical candidate for low-power, persistent machine monitoring.

</details>


### [260] [The Role of Grid Cells in Reducing Spatial Aliasing in Hippocampal Place Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18569)
*Alexander Johnson,Obadah Ghizawi,Ali A. Minai*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究空间混叠问题（多个不同位置产生相似的place-cell表征），提出引入grid cell信号来缓解仅依赖边界向量细胞（BVC）导致的歧义。通过将多个解析构建的grid cell模块与BVC驱动的place cells整合，在三种环境中实现了94-99%的空间混叠减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当环境具有对称性或重复结构时，仅依赖BVC输入构建的位置表征会产生空间混叠——两个或多个不同位置产生高度相似的表征。这种问题在对称或重复结构中尤其严重，因为环境几何特征无法区分感知上相同的位置，导致位置表征的可靠性下降。

Method: 将多个解析构建（analytically constructed）的grid cell模块与BVC驱动的place cells进行整合。Grid cell提供周期性、内部生成的空间信号，这些信号独立于环境几何而变化，因此能够区分感知上相同的位置，从而减轻空间混叠。

Result: 在三种环境中（无障碍物的开放环境、具有十字形中央障碍物（高视觉对称性）的环境、迷宫环境），相比仅用BVC的基线，空间混叠减少了94-99%。在视觉对称性最高的环境中改善最大。

Conclusion: Grid cell提供与边界信息互补的空间信号，能够在地理上模糊的环境中产生更可靠的位置表征，有效缓解仅依赖边界输入导致的空间混叠问题。

Abstract: Spatial aliasing occurs when two or more distinct locations produce highly similar place-cell representations, primarily due to environmental symmetry or repetitive structures. This issue is most pronounced when place representations are constructed solely from boundary vector cell (BVC) inputs, because symmetric or repetitive structures can yield indistinguishable sensory patterns across multiple locations in an environment. This work introduces grid cell signals to mitigate spatial aliasing in such settings. Because grid cells contribute periodic, internally generated spatial signals that vary independently of environmental geometry, they play a key role in disambiguating perceptually identical locations. We integrate multiple modules of analytically constructed grid cells with BVC-driven place cells and show that this leads to a 94--99% reduction in spatial aliasing relative to a BVC-only baseline across three environments: an open environment without obstacles; an environment with a cross-shaped central obstacle creating high visual symmetry; and a maze environment. The greatest improvement occurs in the environment with the highest visual symmetry. These results indicate that grid cells provide information complementary to boundary-based inputs, yielding more reliable place representations in geometrically ambiguous environments.

</details>


### [261] [Biological-Hybrid Intelligence: A Conceptual Framework for Distributed Biological--Artificial Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18748)
*Michael Taynnan Barros,Sergio Lopez Bernal,Reinhold Scherer*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出生物-混合智能（BHI）框架，用于在自适应生物基质与人工基质之间分布式分配计算任务，通过生物电子接口耦合并由协调器管理，定义了三种运行模式并对计算分配、协调和控制进行系统级重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物混合系统分别优化生物基质、AI模型或二者接口，但未明确解决计算如何在两个基质之间分配、重新分配和评估的问题。随着体外神经技术使生物与人工系统双向耦合日益紧密，需要一种系统级框架来回答"计算应驻留在何处"这一关键架构问题。

Method: 提出BHI概念框架：将生物基质和人工基质均视为计算实体，计算职责可在运行中动态变化；要求双向协同适应（区别于仅解码生物活动、仅刺激生物基质或仅适配单一组件的系统）；定义对抗（adversarial）、协作（collaborative）和相互依存（codependent）三种运行模式，分别对应基质间竞争、分工或彼此不可或缺的关系。

Result: BHI为比较不同计算框架提供了共同基础，定义了延迟、活力、接口带宽、学习效率和可复现性等基准指标；同时指出了双向刺激、适应和数据交换带来的治理（governance）考量。

Conclusion: BHI将生物-人工集成重构为计算分配、协调与控制的系统级问题，引导计算机科学界将生物基质视为主动计算资源，并思考计算不仅应如何执行，还应驻留在何处。

Abstract: Biological and artificial systems offer complementary forms of adaptation, learning, and computation, with advances in in-vitro neurotechnology increasingly enabling bidirectional coupling between them. As these systems become more tightly integrated, a key architectural question is how task-relevant computation should be distributed across both substrates. Yet existing biohybrid solutions optimise the biological substrate, the AI model, or their interface without explicitly addressing how such computation is allocated, reassigned, and evaluated. We introduce Biological-Hybrid Intelligence (BHI), a conceptual framework for distributing computation across adaptive biological and artificial substrates coupled through a bioelectronic interface and coordinated by an orchestrator. BHI treats both substrates as computational entities whose computational responsibilities may change during operation. BHI requires reciprocal co-adaptation and differs from systems that merely decode biological activity, stimulate a living substrate, or adapt a single component. BHI further defines three operating modes: adversarial, collaborative, and codependent, distinguished by whether the substrates compete, divide computational labour, or become mutually necessary for task performance. BHI provides a common basis for comparing computational frameworks, defining benchmarks for latency, viability, interface bandwidth, learning efficiency, and reproducibility. It also highlights governance considerations arising from reciprocal stimulation, adaptation, and data exchange. More broadly, BHI invites computer scientists to consider biological substrates as active computational resources and to ask not only how a task should be computed, but where its computation should reside. BHI therefore reframes biological-artificial integration as a system-level problem of computational allocation, coordination, and control.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [Demo: tfdrift - A Severity Taxonomy and Risk Classification Framework for Infrastructure Drift Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18173)
*Sudarshan Bhagvanthakur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对基础设施即代码（IaC）配置漂移的严重性分级分类法，将漂移变化按资源类型和属性级别影响分为四个风险等级，并实现了开源框架tfdrift（含60+可配置规则，覆盖AWS、Azure、GCP）。在150+ AWS Terraform工作空间上的评估显示，严重性过滤可将告警量减少73%，同时保留94%的安全相关变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置漂移（部署的基础设施与声明状态偏离）是云资源管理的持续运营和安全挑战。现有检测方法将所有变更等同对待，导致告警疲劳，使运维人员可能错过安全关键性修改。

Method: 提出了一个通用的严重性分级分类法，根据资源类型和属性级别影响将漂移变更分为四个风险等级；并实现为tfdrift开源框架，包含60多个可配置规则，覆盖AWS、Azure和GCP资源模式（评估以AWS为主）。

Result: 在150+个AWS Terraform工作空间上的评估表明，严重性过滤使告警量减少73%，同时保留94%的安全相关变更，作为基于机器学习告警过滤的轻量级替代方案。

Conclusion: 严重性分级过滤能有效降低告警疲劳同时保持安全关键变化的可见性，为ML方法提供了更轻量、可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: Infrastructure as Code (IaC) tools like Terraform have become the standard for declarative cloud resource management, yet configuration drift, where deployed infrastructure diverges from its declared state, remains a persistent operational and security challenge. Current detection approaches treat all changes equivalently, contributing to alert fatigue that causes operators to miss security-critical modifications. We propose a generalized severity taxonomy for infrastructure drift that classifies changes into four risk tiers based on resource type and attribute-level impact. We implement this taxonomy in tfdrift, an open-source classification framework with 60+ configurable rules covering AWS, Azure, and GCP resource patterns (evaluation reported here is AWS-focused). Evaluation across 150+ AWS Terraform workspaces demonstrates that severity filtering reduces alert volume by 73% while retaining 94% of security-relevant changes, offering a lightweight alternative to ML-based alert filtering. tfdrift is available at github.com/sudarshan8417/tfdrift.

</details>


### [263] [Reproducibility is Not Enough: Artifact Verifiability in Decentralized-Build Package Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18180)
*Oreofe Solarin,Kelechi Kalu,James C. Davis,Paschal Amusuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了四个去中心化包生态系统中软件工件的可验证性，提出独立验证者模型和分层等价性比较模型，并通过工件验证管线测量发现，除构建确定性外，可验证性还受限于缺失的源码/构建元数据、隐式发布转换及非传统构建实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现和可验证的构建能增强分布式软件工件的信任，但工件验证不仅需要确定性构建，还需要恢复源码状态、构建环境、依赖和构建指令。去中心化构建生态系统因异构工具、维护者控制的工作流和碎片化元数据，导致工件是否可独立验证尚不清楚。

Method: 提出独立验证者模型（仅依赖注册表可推导元数据）和分层等价性级别的工件比较模型，并在四个去中心化包生态系统中实现工件验证管线进行测量。

Result: 研究发现，除构建确定性外，可验证性受限于缺失的源码和构建元数据、隐式发布转换以及非传统构建实践。来源证明和嵌入式VCS元数据能改善验证，但无法提供完整的重建规格。

Conclusion: 研究识别出具体的元数据缺口和生态系统级变更需求，以实现在包注册表规模下实用的工件验证。

Abstract: Reproducible and verifiable builds increase trust in distributed software artifacts by enabling independent parties to detect artifacts produced by compromised build or release pipelines. However, artifact verification requires more than deterministic builds: a verifier must also recover the source state, build environment, dependencies, and build instructions that produced the artifact. Decentralized-build ecosystems make this difficult because artifacts are produced through heterogeneous tools, maintainer-controlled workflows, and fragmented metadata. As a result, it remains unclear how often artifacts in these ecosystems can be independently verified.
  This paper studies artifact verifiability across four popular decentralized-build package ecosystems. We define an independent verifier model that relies only on registry-derivable metadata and an artifact comparison model with tiered equivalence levels. We implement these models in an Artifact Verification Pipeline and use it to measure artifact verifiability across the target ecosystems. Our results show that, beyond build determinism, verifiability is limited by missing source and build metadata, implicit release transformations, and unconventional build practices. Provenance attestations and embedded VCS metadata improve verification, but they do not provide complete rebuild specifications. These findings identify concrete metadata gaps and ecosystem-level changes needed to make artifact verification practical at package-registry scale.

</details>


### [264] [What Makes Software Issue Resolution Tasks Difficult for Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18280)
*Ebtesam Al-Haque,Brittany Johnson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个测量框架，用于系统量化软件任务的结构属性与编码智能体成功率之间的关系，通过对最大规模编码智能体轨迹数据集CoderForge-Preview的大规模实证研究，发现任务难度可以从静态特征（AUC=0.863）显著预测，且主要由补丁碎片化和仓库规模驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统在基准测试上快速饱和，但缺乏对任务难度的控制和表征，难以理解什么使任务更难以及难度是否可从静态属性预测。

Method: 在CoderForge-Preview（最大的编码智能体轨迹开源数据集）上进行大规模实证研究，从任务补丁、仓库和提示词提取特征，使用集成方法、SHAP归因和效应量分析评估各特征对任务结果的预测能力。

Result: 任务难度可从静态特征大幅预测（AUC=0.863），主要由补丁碎片化和仓库规模驱动；提示词语言学特征在中档难度任务中成为重要贡献者，揭示难度的分层结构。

Conclusion: 问题解决任务难度编码在其结构中，可实现静态的事前难度估计，为构建难度可控的基准测试评估智能体奠定基础。

Abstract: Background. Advances in agentic systems are simultaneously, and rapidly, saturating benchmarks. Despite this often discussed phenomena, benchmark scores remain difficult to interpret due to the lack of control and characterization of task difficulty. More specifically, we currently have little understanding of what makes one task harder than another, and to what extent task difficulty is predictable from static task properties. Aims. We propose a measurement framework to investigate and systematically quantify what structural properties of software tasks correspond to agent success rates for issue resolution tasks. Method. We conducted a large scale empirical study on CoderForge-Preview, the largest open dataset of coding agent trajectories to date, by extracting features across task patch, repository and prompt. We evaluated the predictive power of each feature against task outcomes using ensemble methods, SHAP attribution, and effect size analysis. Results We found that task difficulty is substantially predictable from static features (AU C = 0.863) and is largely driven by patch fragmentation and repository scale. Prompt linguistic features become visible among top contributors for tasks in the mid-band, revealing a layered structure of difficulty. Conclusion. The difficulty of an issue resolution task is encoded in its structure. This enables static, pre-hoc difficulty estimation and lays the groundwork for difficulty-controlled benchmark construction for evaluation of agents.

</details>


### [265] [Engine-Transfer-Bench: An Evidence-Based Benchmark for Document Compilation Engine Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18329)
*Prajwal S. Venkateshmurthy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Engine-Transfer-Bench (ETB)，一个用于评估和选择文档编译引擎（pdfLaTeX、XeLaTeX、LuaLaTeX、Tectonic、Typst 及 pandoc PDF 后端）的基准框架，包含 1,784 个开源文档、四项任务（可靠性、延迟、文本一致性、失败分析）以及跨操作系统（macOS、Ubuntu、Windows）的托管测试结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏统一的框架来帮助用户在多种文档编译引擎之间进行选择，不同的引擎在可靠性、延迟和兼容性方面表现各异，用户难以做出有依据的决策。

Method: 构建 ETB 基准：包含 1,784 个开源文档，设计四项任务（可靠性、延迟、文本一致性、失败分析），在 GitHub Actions 上对多主机（macOS、Ubuntu、Windows）进行 4,211 次编译测试；引入 S_pdf 文本一致性指标并通过 50 对样本验证（94% 精确率）；提供 ETB-Porta 可移植性门控和跨 OS 公共测试平台。

Result: Tectonic 在跨主机上的成功率稳定在 96.3-97.2%（波动 0.9 个百分点以内），而经典 TeX Live 风格引擎因发行策略（Ubuntu apt、MiKTeX 自动安装、macOS BasicTeX）差异波动达 12-20 个百分点；在 702 个可移植 LaTeX 文档上所有引擎 100% 成功，失败主要集中在 107 个引擎特定模板上；失败本质上是架构性问题（字体、布局、资源），而非缺包问题。

Conclusion: 在可移植文档上，延迟成为引擎选择的主要因素；ETB 提供了可共享的基础设施（基准、可移植性门控和公共测试平台），帮助用户做出更明智的引擎选择决策。

Abstract: There is no shared framework for selecting among document compilation engines (pdfLaTeX, XeLaTeX, LuaLaTeX, Tectonic, Typst, and pandoc PDF backends). We present Engine-Transfer-Bench (ETB): 1,784 open documents, four tasks covering reliability, latency, text consistency, and failures, a pinned harness, and host-tagged multi-OS results. On GitHub Actions (N=4,211 compiles per host across macOS, Ubuntu, and Windows), Tectonic success is stable within 0.9 percentage points (96.3-97.2%), whereas classic TeX Live-style engines vary by 12-20 percentage points according to distribution policy (Ubuntu apt, MiKTeX auto-install, or macOS BasicTeX). On 702 portable LaTeX documents, the tested engines succeed at 100%, making latency the primary selection factor; failures concentrate in 107 engine-specific templates. Within ETB, failures are architectural, involving fonts, layout, and assets, rather than missing packages on a provisioned host. A 50-pair validation of the S_pdf text-consistency metric achieves 94% precision for real content divergence. We release ETB, ETB-Porta, a recommender and portability gate, and a public cross-OS harness as shared infrastructure.

</details>


### [266] [One Gate Is Not Enough: Composing Stateful Pre-Action Controls for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18360)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体AI系统中的控制耦合问题：一个控制器的补救操作可能改变另一个控制器所评估的动作、证据或上下文，使先前判断失效。作者形式化了这种"补救诱导的控制耦合"，提出remediate-and-regate协议在限定条件下恢复每次动作的健全性，并通过有限模型检验证明两种补救算子不可交换。此外，受控证据缓冲区存在投毒漏洞。实证部分在确定性开源工件上验证了CH1-CH6预注册决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统执行后果性动作时受多个预动作控制（权威门、资源门、证据门）同时约束，这些控制可以准入、降级或补救动作。关键问题是：一个控制施加的补救可能改变另一个控制评估的对象（动作、证据或上下文），从而使其先前的判断失效。这种控制间耦合未被形式化，可能导致系统在不健全状态下执行动作。

Method: 1) 形式化"补救诱导的控制耦合"，给出remediate-and-regate协议，在有界、幂等设定下恢复每次动作的健全性；2) 用有限模型检验器寻找两个已实现补救算子（证据替换与资源预算降级）不交换的反例；3) 分析受控证据缓冲区的投毒漏洞并提出两种缓解措施；4) 建立正权重线性聚合门结果补偿成员否决的精确条件；5) 在确定性开源工件上整合三个引擎进行30个预注册种子的实证验证。

Result: 1) remediate-and-regate协议在其假定条件下恢复逐动作健全性；2) 证据替换和资源预算降级两个补救算子不交换，存在具体反例；3) 受控证据缓冲区易受未覆盖缺陷类的投毒攻击，两种缓解措施仅降低而非消除暴露；4) 建立了正权重线性聚合补偿否决的精确条件；5) CH1-CH5在所有30个预注册种子下满足注册决策规则，CH6在W1下满足但在较小的W2工作流下不满足。

Conclusion: 补救顺序属于控制平面语义的一部分而非实现细节；当前admissibility不能保证未来引用的可信度。研究是机制演示（基于开放载荷数据+合成元数据层），而非关于生产环境中普遍性的主张。作者诚实地报告了组合不会产生新的检测覆盖以及CH6在W2下的失败。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems take consequential actions governed by more than one pre-action control at once: authority, resource, and evidence gates that can admit, degrade, or remediate an action before it executes. This paper's central object is remediation-induced control coupling: a remediation applied by one control can change the action, evidence, or context another control evaluates, invalidating that control's earlier judgment. We formalize this coupling and give a remediate-and-regate protocol that restores per-action soundness in the current bounded, idempotent setting under its stated assumptions. We further show that the two implemented remediation operators (evidence substitution and resource-budget downroute) do not commute -- a finite-model checker finds concrete counterexample instances -- making remediation order part of the control-plane semantics rather than an implementation detail. A governed evidence buffer that trusts its own most recent admitted write is a further instance of the same problem at the level of state -- current admissibility does not imply future reference trustworthiness -- and is vulnerable to poisoning from declared-uncovered defect classes; two mitigations reduce, not eliminate, that exposure. Supporting results establish the exact condition under which positive-weight linear aggregation of gate outcomes can compensate a member veto, a unified cross-control Evidence Set, and that composition manufactures no new detection coverage, reported honestly. Empirically, on a deterministic open-data artifact composing three published engines unmodified, CH1-CH5 meet their registered decision rules across all 30 pre-registered seeds; CH6 does so under W1 but not under the smaller W2 workflow, reported as such. This is a mechanism demonstration on open payload data with a synthetic metadata layer, not a claim about production prevalence.

</details>


### [267] [Building real-time digital twin instances with Function+Data Flow: user evaluation and extension for iterative pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18480)
*Eduardo de Conto,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran,Nicholas Ng,Shweta Menon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为Function+Data Flow (FDF)的可视化领域特定语言(DSL)及其集成建模环境DesCartes Builder，用于AI驱动的数字孪生(DT)开发。通过实证用户研究评估了其可用性和功能充分性，结果表明其在目标用户（领域专家）中具有良好可用性。基于研究发现，作者提出了FDF的层次化扩展H-FDF，支持迭代和模块化管道，使AI数字孪生工程走向规范化的建模实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生越来越多地利用AI/ML管道，但从高保真仿真构建实时DT或用历史数据实例化时，管道工程仍然是临时性的：难以指定、验证和复用，缺乏专门工具支持。

Method: 提出FDF（Function+Data Flow）可视化DSL，将函数（ML模型）显式表示以支持组合和复用；在DesCartes Builder集成建模环境中实现；通过实证用户研究评估可用性，并基于反馈提出层次化扩展H-FDF。

Result: 用户研究表明DesCartes Builder和FDF在广泛潜在用户中达到良好可用性水平，尤其适合领域专家。研究揭示了工具和FDF框架的具体优势及改进领域，进而提出了H-FDF扩展。

Conclusion: 集成化、模型驱动的平台是提升AI数字孪生工程质量、使其从临时性开发转变为规范化建模实践的有前景方向。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) increasingly leverage artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) pipelines, both to build real-time DTs from high-fidelity simulations and to instantiate them with historical data. However, engineering these pipelines remains largely ad-hoc: pipelines are hard to specify, validate, and reuse, with scarce dedicated tooling. Function+Data Flow (FDF) addresses this by defining a visual domain-specific language (DSL) that represents functions (ML models) explicitly, enabling their composition and reuse. We implemented FDF in DesCartes Builder, an integrated modeling environment supporting FDF-based DT synthesis and validation.
  In this paper, we report on an empirical user study evaluating whether FDF and DesCartes Builder can make AI-based DT development more accessible and reliable. Participants implemented a representative real-time DT prototype within DesCartes Builder, and we measured perceived usability and feature adequacy through quantitative and qualitative measures. Our results indicate that DesCartes Builder and FDF achieve a good level of usability across a broad range of potential users, and particularly for the intended audience of domain experts. The study additionally surfaces concrete strengths and areas for improvement of both the tool and the underlying FDF framework. Informed by these findings, we propose H-FDF, a Hierarchical extension of FDF supporting iterative and modular pipelines, enabling the formal specification of more complex DT pipelines such as dual training. Our findings suggest that integrated, model-driven platforms are a promising direction to transform AI-based DT engineering into a disciplined modeling practice.

</details>


### [268] [SemaPLC: A Project-Grounded, Verification-Gated Agent Harness for PLC Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18565)
*Yanlun Tu,Huacan Wang,Ziyue Zhou,Jie Zhou,Ningyan Zhu,Ge Chen,Wangyi Chen,Tengfei Zhou,Yifan Zhou,Dasheng Yang,Xiaofeng Mou,Hui Zhang,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了SemaPLC，一个面向可编程逻辑控制器（PLC）代码生成的项目级、验证门控智能体框架。与仅依赖模型自我判断不同，SemaPLC只有在外部日志检查（涵盖规格、编译、运行时行为三个层面）确认通过后才宣布任务完成。在117个独立POU任务上达到72.6%的平均严格验证通过率（7个模型中最高），在65个需集成到真实项目的任务中同样取得最高平均分。动态行为验证最具区分度：基线方法动态得分在22.4-31.4之间，而SemaPLC达到52.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型已经能生成独立的PLC程序组织单元（POU），但这些生成的逻辑能否真正集成到现有PLC项目中并正确运行，仅在有限的测试中得到了验证。大多数方法在模型自我评估足够时就停止，缺乏严格的、基于外部验证的任务完成标准。

Method: SemaPLC是一个项目级且验证门控的智能体框架，由传统工具组装而成但受严格完成规则约束。任务完成不仅依赖模型自我判断，还必须在外部日志检查确认后宣布。检查覆盖三个层面：规格（specification）、编译（compilation）以及真实运行时的行为（behavior）。运行时行为通过将生成逻辑和参考逻辑部署到真实PLC运行时并比较执行轨迹来衡量。

Result: 在117个独立POU任务上，SemaPLC在全部7个模型中取得最高的严格验证通过率（均值72.6%）。在65个需要集成到真实项目的任务上，在集成编译、静态行为和动态行为三个指标上均取得最高均值。动态行为最具区分度：所有方法的静态得分差距在10个百分点以内，而动态得分差距显著，基线为22.4-31.4，SemaPLC达到52.2。

Conclusion: 验证门控框架在每个层面都提升了平均表现，且在运行时层面的提升最为显著。执行（运行），而非静态评分，才是测试生成控制逻辑是否真正有效的忠实标准。SemaPLC已开源。

Abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) run industrial plants, and large language models can already generate independent program organization units (POUs) for them. Whether such logic integrates into an existing PLC project and then runs correctly has been checked only in limited tests. We present \textsc{SemaPLC}, a project-grounded and verification-gated agent harness assembled from conventional tools but governed by a strict completion rule. Rather than stopping when the model judges its own output adequate, \textsc{SemaPLC} declares a task complete only when logged external checks confirm it. Those checks cover the specification, the compilation, and the behavior on a live runtime. On 117 independent-POU tasks matching existing benchmarks, it attains the highest strict verified pass rate on all seven models (72.6\% mean). On a project-context track of 65 tasks whose generated logic must compile and run inside a real project, it attains the highest mean on integrated compilation, static behavior, and dynamic behavior. Of the three layers, dynamic behavior is the most revealing. We measure it by deploying the generated and the reference logic to a live PLC runtime and comparing their executed traces. All methods fall within 10 static points of one another, whereas dynamic scores separate them sharply, from 22.4 to 31.4 for the baselines against 52.2 for \textsc{SemaPLC}. Overall, our verification-gated harness raises the mean at every layer and most sharply at runtime. Execution, not static scoring, is the faithful test of whether generated control logic actually works. \textsc{SemaPLC} is open-sourced at https://github.com/midea-ai/SemaPLC.

</details>


### [269] [AppEval: A Unified Benchmark for LLM-Based Mobile Application Repair in ArkTS, Swift, and Kotlin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18588)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhenyu Shi,Yonghao Zhang,Senjian Zhang,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Chenhao Ying,Yuan Luo,Wei Chen,Haiming Jin,Shaocong Long,Xu Liu,Zhe Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AppEval是一个面向移动应用修复的基准测试和原生工具链评估框架，覆盖HarmonyOS/ArkTS、iOS/Swift和Android/Kotlin三大平台。它通过隐藏行为测试与参考生产修复分离的方式，要求修复只有在同一安装应用目标在缺陷版本上达到断言失败、修复后通过时才被接受，并将基础设施故障区分为独立结果。在200个经审计的Android任务上，五个智能体的Pass@1在22.00%到90.50%之间，展示了68.50个百分点的显著差距，证实了移动修复性能强烈依赖被评估的智能体，同时也说明了运行时感知验收对有意义比较的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级LLM智能体评估通常只在构建主机上运行测试，未能覆盖移动应用特有的"构建-安装-启动-测试"边界。在真实移动环境中，缺失SDK、设备离线或断言前崩溃等基础设施问题常被误认为程序失败，导致评估结果失真且不同平台间缺乏可比性。

Method: AppEval将每个任务的隐藏行为测试与参考生产修复分离，仅当同一安装应用目标在缺陷版本上达到断言失败且修复后通过时才判定任务被接受，基础设施失败作为独立结果处理。通过统一schema映射到各平台的构建系统、运行时和测试运行器，并构建了包含200个可独立构建仓库的已审计Android测试集。

Result: 在200个Android任务上，五个智能体的Pass@1范围为22.00%到90.50%，性能差距高达68.50个百分点。该结果表明移动修复性能主要取决于被评估的智能体能力，而非任务本身的难度。

Conclusion: 运行时感知的验收机制（区分真实程序失败与基础设施故障）对移动应用修复评估至关重要，否则无法进行有意义的跨智能体比较。当前定量结论仅限Android平台，需在审计后的iOS和HarmonyOS结果完成后才能得出跨平台泛化结论。

Abstract: Repository-level LLM agents are typically evaluated on projects whose tests run on the build host. It remains unclear whether their repairs survive the mobile build-install-launch-test boundary, where a missing SDK, offline device, or pre-assertion crash can be mistaken for a program failure. We present AppEval, a benchmark and native-toolchain evaluation framework for mobile application repair across HarmonyOS/ArkTS, iOS/Swift, and Android/Kotlin. Each task separates a hidden behavior test from the reference production fix and is accepted only when the same installed-app target reaches an assertion failure on the defective revision and passes after the fix; infrastructure failures remain a distinct outcome. A common schema maps this contract to each platform's build system, runtime, and test runner. The audited Android partition contains 200 accepted instrumentation tasks from 24 independently buildable repositories. On these tasks, five agents achieve Pass@1 between 22.00% and 90.50%, a 68.50-percentage-point spread under the same dynamic oracle. These results show that mobile repair performance depends strongly on the evaluated agent while demonstrating why runtime-aware acceptance is necessary for meaningful comparison. The quantitative findings in this paper are Android-specific; audited iOS and HarmonyOS results are required before drawing cross-platform generalization conclusions.

</details>


### [270] [OdinEval: A Reproducible Benchmark for LLM-Based Program Repair in the Odin Programming Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18595)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Senjian Zhang,Yuan Luo,Chenhao Ying,Haiming Jin,Wei Chen,Shaocong Long,Zhenyu Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了OdinEval，一个面向Odin系统编程语言的可复现仓库级缺陷修复基准，包含168个实例，并评估了六个语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级修复基准集中在主流语言上，系统编程语言（如Odin）缺乏测试覆盖。

Method: 从公开Odin仓库收集有文档记录的缺陷，每个实例绑定issue、提交、金补丁、回归测试、历史工具链和执行记录。无开发者测试时使用黑盒测试，由同一模型三个实例独立审查，并在历史状态下执行，根据记录的反馈和版本化测试编写技能进行修订。

Result: 在168个过滤后的实例上评估六个语言模型，Kimi-K3达到最高Resolved分数66.7%，Qwen3.8-Max达到最高Repro分数96.4%。

Conclusion: OdinEval填补了系统编程语言在仓库级修复基准中的空白，提供了包含冻结数据、容器、验证器等在内的完整可复现资源。

Abstract: Repository-level repair benchmarks still center on a few mainstream languages, leaving systems languages such as Odin largely untested. We present OdinEval, a reproducible benchmark built from documented defects in public Odin repositories. Each instance binds an issue to base and fix commits, a gold patch, an issue-specific regression test, a historical toolchain, and execution records. Admission requires the test to fail on the base revision and pass after the gold fix. When no usable developer test exists, a black-box test is reviewed independently by three instances of the same model, executed in both historical states, and revised from recorded feedback under a versioned Test Writing Skill.
