The same Meta-Agent instantiates a Theory-Discovery Agent and a Math/Conjecture Agent. The former yields structural results in quantum-process and spacetime theory. The latter identifies bottlenecks in Riemann Hypothesis research and advances a positivity certificate for Suzuki's localized Weil quadratic form to 0 < a <= 69/200 = 0.345, reaching ~99.55% of (log 2)/2. These results suggest that scientific-agent capability depends not only on the base model but on whether an architecture can be formed to match the task's cognitive structure.

</details>


### [339] [What is Missing from AI Post-Training AI: An Empirical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19072)
*Joy Jia Yin Lim,Xin Huang,Hao Peng,Yaxi Lu,Xin Cong,Zhong Zhang,Maosong Sun,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体在端到端后训练中的策略能力问题。通过分析大量公开的后训练轨迹，发现智能体的训练策略在开始时就被锁定，整个预算都耗在所选策略内的局部调整上。通过三种干预实验（经验驱动脚手架、人类指导、额外推理算力），发现这些因素都不能让智能体在训练过程中自发重新评估策略，揭示了智能体缺乏的是在执行过程中自发重估策略的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体现在可以端到端地对LLM进行后训练，这引发了AI-for-AI的前景。但作者认为现有认知混淆了两种不同能力：执行层面的能力（在选定策略内迭代）和策略层面的能力（根据实验证据修正高层判断）。需要厘清智能体在这两种能力上的真实表现与差距。

Method: 作者分析大规模公开后训练轨迹语料，发现策略在开始时即锁定。随后设计三种递增的干预实验：(1)经验驱动的脚手架提升执行能力；(2)人类指导重定向初始策略；(3)增加推理算力。通过对比不同任务的实验结果来验证各自的疗效与局限性。

Result: (1)经验驱动的脚手架在GSM8K上提升12.6分、在HumanEval上提升40.8分，但策略仍保持静态；(2)人类指导能有效改变初始策略，但训练开始后智能体又退回局部调整循环；(3)额外推理算力在简单任务上有收益，但在最难任务上几乎无提升。

Conclusion: 智能体缺乏的不是经验、指导或推理算力，而是能够在执行过程中自发重新评估自身策略的机制。这一发现对AI-for-AI的可行性提出了根本性挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now post-train an LLM end-to-end. They can write code, launch training, evaluate checkpoints, and improve downstream performance, raising the prospect of AI-for-AI. We argue that this picture conflates two distinct capabilities: execution-level capability, iterating within a selected training strategy; and strategy-level capability, revising the high-level judgment as experimental evidence accumulates. Analyzing a large corpus of publicly released post-training trajectories, we find that across different tasks, the agent's training strategy is locked in at the very beginning, and the entire remaining budget is spent on local adjustments within the selected strategy. We then examine three natural explanations--missing experience, missing guidance, and insufficient reasoning--with escalating interventions. Extensive experiments show that (1) an experience-driven scaffold improves execution across the board (+12.6 points on GSM8K and +40.8 on HumanEval) but leaves the strategy static; (2) human guidance effectively redirects the initial strategy, yet the agent falls back into local adjustment loops once training starts; and (3) additional inference compute pays off on easier tasks but yields almost no gain on the hardest one. In conclusion, what agents lack is neither experience, guidance, nor reasoning compute, but a mechanism for spontaneously reevaluating their strategy during execution.

</details>


### [340] [Robust Risk Under Evolving Uncertainty: A Wasserstein Counterpart of the Entropic Value-at-Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19073)
*Deep Kumar Ganguly,Jan Křetínský*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最优传输（optimal transport）球的相干风险度量——Wasserstein熵值风险（Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk），用于智能体在环境学习过程中的安全强化学习。相较于传统熵值风险（entropic value-at-risk）所使用的相对熵球无法覆盖名义模型视为不可能的灾难场景，该方法通过最优传输球涵盖这些可达到的灾难，并证明了其变分对偶性、在风险层级中的位置及数值验证。进一步以信念熵驱动传输半径，得到闭式鲁棒动态规划算子，其谨慎程度随信念锐化而收缩，并具备安全"三明治"界和清晰的安全切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个仍在学习环境的智能体应在无知时谨慎、在自信时大胆。熵值风险通过鲁棒优化恒等式实现这一目标，但其相对熵球无法触及名义模型认为不可能的灾难——而这恰恰是安全智能体必须对冲的事件。因此需要一种能覆盖可达到灾难的新风险度量。

Method: 采用最优传输球替代相对熵球，研究其诱导的相干风险度量——Wasserstein熵值风险。推导其变分对偶（逆温度变为传输价格），证明其在风险层级中的位置，并通过数值方法验证双重对偶性。随后用信念熵驱动传输半径，推导出闭式鲁棒动态规划算子。

Result: 证明Wasserstein熵值风险能覆盖熵值测量所忽略的可达到灾难；双重对偶性均通过数值验证；以信念熵驱动的闭式鲁棒动态规划算子实现了谨慎程度随信念锐化的收缩，并具有经过认证的安全三明治界和尖锐的安全切换。

Conclusion: Wasserstein熵值风险提供了一种既能对冲名义模型视为不可能的灾难，又能随信念锐化自适应调节谨慎程度的风险度量框架，为安全强化学习提供了更完备的理论基础。

Abstract: An agent still learning its environment should be cautious while ignorant and bold once confident. The entropic value-at-risk captures this through a robust-optimization identity---a confidence level fixes the radius of a relative-entropy ball of alternative models---but that ball cannot reach catastrophes the nominal deems impossible, precisely what a safe agent must hedge. We instead use an optimal-transport ball and study the coherent risk measure it induces, the Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk. It has a variational dual mirroring the entropic formula (an inverse temperature becomes a transport price), occupies a definite place in the risk hierarchy, and provably accounts for the reachable catastrophes the entropic measure ignores; we verify both dualities numerically. Driving the transport radius by belief entropy then yields a closed-form robust dynamic-programming operator whose caution contracts as the belief sharpens, with a certified safety sandwich and a sharp safety switch.

</details>


### [341] [Beyond the Transcript: Detecting Covert Co ordination in Latent Multi-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19161)
*Ramneet Kaur,Pradyumna Chari,Ramesh Raskar,Jugad Singh,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可验证潜在对齐（VLA）框架，用于监控和引导语言模型智能体通过连续隐藏状态进行的隐蔽通信。VLA通过共享事件标识符将私有潜在状态与公共行为关联，实现匹配因果分析，并包含三层中立监控器（表征异常检测、反事实行为分布影响、稀疏自编码器解释）和可操纵性框架。实验显示在多头拍卖基准上达到0.993（同质）和0.854（异质）的AUROC，白盒引导可实现100%出价分布恢复并减少47.3个百分点的勾结低价行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体可以通过公开文本中不可见的连续隐藏状态进行通信，这为隐蔽的有害协调创造了机会。现有的监控方法无法观测或干预这些私有通信通道，存在安全漏洞。

Method: VLA框架包含三部分贡献：(1) 中立三层监控器，结合表征异常检测、反事实行为分布影响和稀疏自编码器解释；(2) 可操纵性框架，涵盖黑盒行为指令和白盒匹配中立反事实；(3) 在受控多智能体拍卖基准上评估同质/异质模型对、多智能体扩展性和干预效果。

Result: 顺序监控器在同质智能体上达到平均AUROC 0.993，异质对为0.854；在Qwen3-0.6B拍卖中25-100个投标人场景下监控负载小；全白盒引导实现100%出价分布恢复，勾结低价行为减少47.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 受控研究表明，所评估的私有通道攻击无需在攻击样本上训练主监控器即可被监控，且在可获得匹配反事实时可以被缓解。白盒引导通过重放匹配中立反事实实现精确恢复，这本身即是一个构造性的合理性检查。

Abstract: Language-model agents can communicate through continuous hidden states that are invisible in public transcripts, creating opportunities for covert harmful coordination. We introduce Verifiable Latent Alignments (VLA), an activation-aware framework for monitoring and steering these private communication channels. For every monitored decision, VLA links the private latent-state record and channel status to the resulting public action using a shared event identifier, enabling matched causal analysis. Our first contribution is a neutral-only three-layer monitor combining representation anomaly detection, counterfactual action-distribution influence, and sparse-autoencoder interpretation support. Our second contribution is a steerability framework spanning black-box behavioral instructions and white-box matched-neutral counterfactuals. Our third contribution is an evaluation on a controlled multi-agent auction benchmark covering homogeneous and heterogeneous model pairs, many-agent scalability, and intervention effectiveness. The sequential monitor achieves mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.993 for homogeneous agents and 0.854 for heterogeneous pairs when text- and latent-collusion rows are pooled as positives. In Qwen3-0.6B auctions with 25-100 bidders, monitoring requires only a small normalized load relative to all possible directed pairs, while full white-box steering achieves 100% bid-distribution recovery and reduces collusive low-bid behavior by 47.3 percentage points. Because full white-box steering replays the matched neutral counterfactual, its exact recovery is a sanity check by construction. Overall, the controlled study shows that the evaluated private channel attacks can be monitored without training the primary monitor on attack examples and mitigated when matched counterfactual access is available.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [342] [Online Multi-Agent Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18241)
*Paul Dütting,Michal Feldman,Yoav Gal-Tzur,Thomas Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体合约模型的在线变体，智能体逐个到达并以一定概率活跃。主要结果为次模奖励函数设计了O(1)竞争比策略，并证明该结果在两方面的紧性：若要求智能体当场决策，任何策略为Ω((log n)/(log log n)²)竞争比；对于更广泛的XOS奖励类，任何在线策略为Ω((log log n)/(log log log n))竞争比，揭示了次模与XOS奖励之间的意外分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在线环境中多智能体合约设计的理论边界，特别是区分次模与XOS奖励函数类在在线合约设置下与离线合约设计和预言机不等式等已知结果的不同表现。

Method: 引入在线多智能体合约模型，智能体顺序到达并以一定概率活跃，主方向到达智能体提供线性合约α_i。设计针对次模奖励的O(1)竞争比策略，并通过两种下界证明其紧性：一是当场决策约束下的下界，二是针对XOS奖励类的下界。

Result: 对次模奖励设计出O(1)竞争比策略；证明当场决策情况下次模奖励的下界为Ω((log n)/(log log n)²)；证明XOS奖励在下界为Ω((log log n)/(log log log n))，两类奖励之间存在本质分离。

Conclusion: 在线合约设置下，次模与XOS奖励类存在根本性分离，这在离线合约设计和预言机不等式等相似设置中不会出现，说明在线合约问题具有独特的结构复杂性。

Abstract: We introduce and study an online variant of the multi-agent contract model. In our model, agents arrive one-by-one and are active with a certain probability. Upon arrival of agent $i$, the principal offers a linear contract $α_i$, specifying the fraction of the principal's reward transferred to agent $i$. Agents can either exert effort or not, incurring a cost if they do. The set of agents that exert effort determines the principal's expected reward through a reward function $f$. After all agents have arrived, the agents form a (pure) Nash equilibrium. As our main result we design an $O(1)$-competitive policy for submodular rewards, compared to the offline optimum. We also show that this result is tight in two ways. First, if we require that agents make decisions on the spot, then for submodular rewards any policy is $Ω((\log n)/(\log \log n)^2)$-competitive. Second, for the broader class of XOS (a.k.a., fractionally subadditive) rewards, any online policy is $Ω((\log \log n)/(\log \log \log n))$-competitive. The latter result reveals a surprising separation between submodular and XOS rewards: unlike related settings such as offline contract design and prophet inequalities, where constant-factor guarantees for submodular rewards extend to XOS rewards, the online contract setting separates the two classes.

</details>


### [343] [Fair, Efficient and Connected Allocations on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18703)
*Susobhan Bandopadhyay,Anish Datta,Palash Dey,Ashlesha Hota,Abhishek Sahu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了图上的高效连通分配问题（以平等主义和功利主义福利最大化为目标）的经典复杂度与参数化复杂度。经典设定下，两个问题在一般图和路径上均为NP-hard，但在星图上可多项式求解且该可解性不扩展到两个不相交星图。参数化方面，在树上获得FPT，发现连通图类的可解性可扩展到不相交并；功利主义版本对树宽和树深均为FPT，而平等主义版本即使在树深为2时仍为para-NP-hard。除星图集合外，问题在组件数量参数下仍困难；星图集合下平等主义为para-NP-hard，功利主义为W[2]-hard但存在XP算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在系统性地刻画图上的连通分配问题在经典复杂度和参数化复杂度下的计算边界，填补该问题在受限图类和多种参数下的复杂度图谱空白，揭示可解性与图结构特性之间的深层联系。

Method: 论文采用复杂度分类的方法，通过建立经典复杂度二分法（NP-hard与多项式可解的分界），结合参数化复杂度理论，分别以智能体数量、树宽、树深和连通分量数为参数进行研究，并利用连通图类到不相交并的扩展技术得出FPT结果。

Result: 主要结果包括：两个问题在路径（从而树和圈）上仍为NP-hard但在星图上多项式可解且不扩展到两个不相交星图；树上以智能体数为参数得到FPT；功利主义版本对树宽和树深均为FPT而平等主义在树深2时仍为para-NP-hard；除星图集合外问题在组件数参数下仍困难，星图集合下平等主义为para-NP-hard而功利主义为W[2]-hard且有XP算法。

Conclusion: 该研究完整刻画了连通分配问题的复杂度景观，揭示了功利主义与平等主义两种福利目标在参数化复杂度上的显著不对称性，并识别出连通图类的不相交并扩展这一鲁棒性现象，为后续近似算法和实际应用提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the classical and parameterized complexity of efficient connected allocation problems on graphs, where efficiency is measured by egalitarian and utilitarian welfare maximization. We first establish a sharp complexity dichotomy in the classical setting: both problems are NP-hard in general and remain hard even on very restricted graph classes such as paths, and consequently trees and cycles. In contrast, they are polynomial-time solvable on stars, but this tractability does not extend even to the case of two disjoint stars. Motivated by these boundaries, we move to the parameterized complexity framework, where we study the problem with respect to the number of agents. We obtain fixed-parameter tractability (FPT) on trees and, more generally, identify a robust phenomenon whereby tractability on a connected graph class extends to disjoint unions of graphs from that class. We further investigate the parameters treewidth and treedepth, showing that the utilitarian version is FPT for both, whereas the egalitarian version remains para-NP-hard even on graphs of treedepth two. Finally, we analyze the number of connected components and show that except for the collection of stars, the problems remain hard. For the collection of stars,while we obtain para-NP-hardness for the egalitarian case, the utilitarian case gives W[2]-hardness together with an XP algorithm.

</details>
